  ∃ K,
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.QuadraticPhaseCell h m K ∧
      Fintype.card (PhonologicalCalculus.Application.QuadraticHorizonCode K) = K + 1 ∧
        (∀ (horizon : ℕ),
            PhonologicalCalculus.Application.quadraticHorizonDecode h m K
                (PhonologicalCalculus.Application.quadraticHorizonEncode K horizon) =
              PhonologicalCalculus.Application.quadraticDeclaredHorizonProfile h m K horizon) ∧
          (∀ (horizon : ℕ), PhonologicalCalculus.Application.QuadraticHorizonOptimizerProof h m K horizon) ∧
            ∀ (horizon : ℕ), PhonologicalCalculus.Application.QuadraticHorizonProfileProof h m K horizon

            k = ⟨10, ⋯⟩)) ∧
      41 / 42 ≠ 1

PhonologicalCalculus.Context.ctx_c1_general_04_analytic {h m p : ℝ} {K : ℕ}
  (hcell : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell h m p K) :
  ∃! u,
    u ∈ Set.Ioc 0 1 ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.Context.shiftedPowerSum K (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) u =
          (p * (h / m)) ^ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p ∧
        (∀ (L : ℕ),
            ∃ winner,
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteIntervalBox
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.absoluteIntervalPathHarmony h m p) winner ∧
                (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteIntervalAllPositive winner ↔
                    L ≤
                      PhonologicalCalculus.Context.maximumPositiveGap K
                        (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.absoluteEdgeSupportClass u)) ∧
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteIntervalHasZero winner ↔
                    PhonologicalCalculus.Context.maximumPositiveGap K
                        (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.absoluteEdgeSupportClass u) <
                      L)) ∧
          (∀ L ≤ 2 * K - 1,
              ∃ winner,
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteIntervalBox
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.absoluteIntervalPathHarmony h m p) winner ∧
                  PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteIntervalAllPositive winner) ∧
            (∃ winner,
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteIntervalBox
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.absoluteIntervalPathHarmony h m p) winner ∧
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteIntervalHasZero winner ↔ u ≤ 1 / 2)) ∧
              (∀ (L : ℕ),
                  2 * K + 1 ≤ L →
                    ∃ winner,
                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn
                          PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteIntervalBox
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.absoluteIntervalPathHarmony h m p) winner ∧
                        PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteIntervalHasZero winner) ∧
                ∀ (v : ℝ),
                  PhonologicalCalculus.Context.phaseBit u = PhonologicalCalculus.Context.phaseBit v →
                    ∀ (L : ℕ),
                      PhonologicalCalculus.Context.positiveAtGap K L
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.absoluteEdgeSupportClass u) =
                        PhonologicalCalculus.Context.positiveAtGap K L
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.absoluteEdgeSupportClass v)

PhonologicalCalculus.Context.ctx_c2_general_03_analytic {h m p : ℝ} {K : ℕ}
  (hcell : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell h m p K) (hKtwo : 2 ≤ K) :
  (∀ (profile : List ℝ),
      PhonologicalCalculus.Context.IsGlobalWinner
          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.powerPenalty p) h m) profile ↔
        PhonologicalCalculus.Context.IsGlobalWinner
          (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.absolutePathHarmony (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.powerPenalty p) h m)
          profile) ∧
    (∀ (L : ℕ),
        0 < L →
          ((∃ winner,
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.directionalIntervalPathHarmony h m p) winner ∧
                ∀ (i : ℕ), 0 < PhonologicalCalculus.Context.directionalReconstructedActivity winner i) ↔
            PhonologicalCalculus.Context.positiveAtGap K L PhonologicalCalculus.Context.EdgeSupportClass.forward =
              true)) ∧
      ∃! u,
        u ∈ Set.Ioc 0 1 ∧
          PhonologicalCalculus.Context.shiftedPowerSum K (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) u =
              (p * (h / m)) ^ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p ∧
            (∀ (L : ℕ),
                (∃ winner,
                    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn
                        PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteIntervalBox
                        (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.absoluteIntervalPathHarmony h m p) winner ∧
                      PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteIntervalAllPositive winner) ↔
                  PhonologicalCalculus.Context.positiveAtGap K L
                      (PhonologicalCalculus.Context.absoluteEdgeSupportClass u) =
                    true) ∧
              (∀ (L : ℕ),
                  0 < L →
                    L < K + 1 →
                      PhonologicalCalculus.Context.DirectionalGapPositive h m p L ∧
                        PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteGapPositive h m p L) ∧
                ¬PhonologicalCalculus.Context.DirectionalGapPositive h m p (K + 1) ∧
                  PhonologicalCalculus.Context.AbsoluteGapPositive h m p (K + 1)

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b10_complete_global_comparative_statics :
  (∀ (p rhoOne rhoTwo : ℝ) (K L : ℕ),
      1 < p →
        0 < rhoOne →
          0 < rhoTwo →
            rhoOne ≤ rhoTwo →
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell rhoOne 1 p K →
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell rhoTwo 1 p L →
                  ∃ etaOne etaTwo,
                    0 ≤ etaOne ∧
                      etaOne < 1 ∧
                        0 ≤ etaTwo ∧
                          etaTwo < 1 ∧
                            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass rhoOne 1 p etaOne K = 1 ∧
                              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass rhoTwo 1 p etaTwo L = 1 ∧
                                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn
                                    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoOne 1 p
                                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight K))
                                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease rhoOne 1 p etaOne K) ∧
                                  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn
                                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoTwo 1 p
                                        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight L))
                                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease rhoTwo 1 p etaTwo L) ∧
                                    ∀ (i : ℕ),
                                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
                                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease rhoOne 1 p etaOne K) i ≤
                                        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
                                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease rhoTwo 1 p etaTwo L) i) ∧
    (∀ (h m p : ℝ) (K : ℕ),
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell h m p K →
          PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerStablePositiveHorizon h m p K ∧
            (∀ (J : ℕ), 0 < J → J < K → ¬PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerStablePositiveHorizon h m p J) ∧
              ∃ eta,
                0 ≤ eta ∧
                  eta < m ∧
                    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass h m p eta K = 1 ∧
                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn
                          PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective h m p
                            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight K))
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p eta K) ∧
                        ∀ (R : ℕ),
                          PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn
                              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective h m p
                                (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight (K + R)))
                              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p (eta + m * ↑R) (K + R)) ∧
                            ∀ (i : ℕ),
                              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
                                  (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p (eta + m * ↑R) (K + R)) i =
                                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
                                  (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p eta K) i) ∧
      (∀ (rhoOne rhoTwo theta p : ℝ) (N : ℕ),
          0 < rhoOne →
            0 < rhoTwo →
              1 < p →
                0 < N →
                  0 ≤ theta →
                    theta ≤ 1 →
                      ∃ winnerOne winnerTwo winnerMiddle,
                        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn
                            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoOne 1 p
                              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N))
                            winnerOne ∧
                          PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn
                              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoTwo 1 p
                                (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N))
                              winnerTwo ∧
                            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn
                                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                                (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective
                                  (theta * rhoOne + (1 - theta) * rhoTwo) 1 p
                                  (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N))
                                winnerMiddle ∧
                              theta *
                                    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoOne 1 p
                                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N) winnerOne +
                                  (1 - theta) *
                                    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoTwo 1 p
                                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N) winnerTwo ≤
                                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective
                                  (theta * rhoOne + (1 - theta) * rhoTwo) 1 p
                                  (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N) winnerMiddle) ∧
        ∀ (q : ℝ) (K i : ℕ),
          0 < q →
            0 < K →
              Filter.Tendsto (fun tau => PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile q tau (K + 1) i)
                (nhdsWithin 1 (Set.Iio 1)) (nhds (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile q 0 K i))

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b11_complete_query_relative_identifiability {p rho lambda h m : ℝ}
  {decrease : ℕ → ℝ} {K i j t : ℕ}
  (hadmit : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.PoweredGapFamilyAdmission p rho decrease K) (hij : i < j) (hjK : j < K)
  (htK : t + 2 < K) (hc : 0 < decrease (t + 2)) (hcb : decrease (t + 2) < decrease (t + 1))
  (hba : decrease (t + 1) < decrease t) (hLogConcave : decrease t * decrease (t + 2) < decrease (t + 1) ^ 2)
  (hlambda : 0 < lambda) (hm : 0 < m) :
  rho = ↑(j - i) / (p * (decrease i ^ (p - 1) - decrease j ^ (p - 1))) ∧
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.TripleExponentRatioSystem (decrease t) (decrease (t + 1)) (decrease (t + 2)) p
        rho ∧
      (∀ (parameter : ℝ × ℝ),
          PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.TripleExponentRatioSystem (decrease t) (decrease (t + 1)) (decrease (t + 2))
              parameter.1 parameter.2 →
            parameter = (p, rho)) ∧
        lambda * h / (lambda * m) = h / m
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b11_phase_01 :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.QuadraticPhaseCell 5 1 4 ∧ 3 < 5 / 1 ∧ 5 / 1 ≤ 5
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b11_ratio_02 :
  have d₁ := 2 / 5;
  have d₂ := 3 / 10;
  1 / (2 * (d₁ - d₂)) = 5
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b11_scale_04 (x y : List ℝ) :
  35 / 7 = 5 / 1 ∧
    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPenalty 35 7 x ≤
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPenalty 35 7 y ↔
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPenalty 5 1 x ≤
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPenalty 5 1 y)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b11_triple_03 :
  have a := 3 / 5;
  have b := 1 / 2;
  have c := 2 / 5;
  (a / b) ^ 0 + (c / b) ^ 0 = 2 ∧ (a / b) ^ 1 + (c / b) ^ 1 = 2 ∧ b ^ 2 > a * c

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b12_exactUniformSimplexLattice_selectedMinimizers.{u_1} {N : ℕ} {β : Type u_1}
  {objective : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ} {optimizer : Fin N → ℝ} {query : (Fin N → ℝ) → β} (hcontinuous : Continuous objective)
  (hunique :
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueCompactMinimizerOn {d | PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex d}
      objective optimizer)
  (habsent : ∀ (n : ℕ), optimizer ∉ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformSimplexLattice n)
  (hlocal : ∀ᶠ (x : Fin N → ℝ) in nhds optimizer, query x = query optimizer) :
  Filter.Tendsto (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exactUniformSimplexLatticeMinimizer objective hcontinuous)
      Filter.atTop (nhds optimizer) ∧
    (∀ (n : ℕ),
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exactUniformSimplexLatticeMinimizer objective hcontinuous n ≠ optimizer) ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
        query (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exactUniformSimplexLatticeMinimizer objective hcontinuous n) =
          query optimizer
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b12_lattice_convergence_without_identity.{u_1, u_2} {α : Type u_1} {β : Type u_2}
  [PseudoMetricSpace α] {carrier : Set α} {objective : α → ℝ} {optimizer : α} {lattice : ℕ → Set α}
  {comparison latticeMinimizer : ℕ → α} {query : α → β} (hcompact : IsCompact carrier)
  (hcontinuous : Continuous objective)
  (hunique : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueCompactMinimizerOn carrier objective optimizer)
  (hcomparisonCarrier : ∀ (n : ℕ), comparison n ∈ carrier) (hlatticeCarrier : ∀ (n : ℕ), latticeMinimizer n ∈ carrier)
  (hcomparison : Filter.Tendsto comparison Filter.atTop (nhds optimizer))
  (hoptimal : ∀ (n : ℕ), comparison n ∈ carrier → objective (latticeMinimizer n) ≤ objective (comparison n))
  (habsent : ∀ (n : ℕ), optimizer ∉ lattice n) (hmember : ∀ (n : ℕ), latticeMinimizer n ∈ lattice n)
  (hlocal : ∀ᶠ (x : α) in nhds optimizer, query x = query optimizer) :
  Filter.Tendsto latticeMinimizer Filter.atTop (nhds optimizer) ∧
    (∀ (n : ℕ), latticeMinimizer n ≠ optimizer) ∧
      ∀ᶠ (n : ℕ) in Filter.atTop, query (latticeMinimizer n) = query optimizer

                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tau K i -
                        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.scalingProfile p (↑i / ↑K)| <
                    epsilon) ∧

          ∀ (tau : ℝ),

                  1 ≤ i →
                    i ≤ K →
                      |↑K *
                              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseDecrease
                                (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tau K (K - i) -
                            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.scalingDensity p (↑i / ↑K)| <
                        epsilon

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b3_complete_quadratic_active_set.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι] (weight : ι → ℝ)
  {rho₁ rho₂ : ℝ} (hrho₁ : rho₁ ≠ 0) (hrho₂ : rho₂ ≠ 0) :
  (∀ (h m : ℝ) (N : ℕ),
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.QuadraticUnsaturatedCell h m N →
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticReducedObjective h m
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPathWeight N))
            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticUnsaturatedDecrease h m N) ∧
          (∀ (i : Fin N),
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticUnsaturatedProfile h m N ↑i -
                  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticUnsaturatedProfile h m N (↑i + 1) =
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticUnsaturatedDecrease h m N i) ∧
            0 < PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticUnsaturatedProfile h m N N) ∧
    (∀ (h m : ℝ) (K : ℕ),
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.QuadraticPhaseCell h m K →
          PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticReducedObjective h m
                (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPathWeight K))
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticSaturatedDecrease h m K) ∧
            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticSaturatedProfile h m K K = 0 ∧
                (∀ i < K, 0 < PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticSaturatedProfile h m K i) ∧
                  ∀ (i : ℕ), K ≤ i → PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticSaturatedProfile h m K i = 0) ∧
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.QuadraticThresholdReached h m K ∧
                  ∀ j < K, ¬PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.QuadraticThresholdReached h m j) ∧
                ∀ (R : ℕ),
                  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticReducedObjective h m
                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPathWeight (K + R)))
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticExtendedDecrease h m K R)) ∧
      ∑ i, (weight i / (2 * rho₁) - weight i / (2 * rho₂)) ^ 2 = (∑ i, weight i ^ 2) * (1 / rho₁ - 1 / rho₂) ^ 2 / 4

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b4_endpoint_obstruction {phi : ℝ → ℝ} {h m : ℝ} (hh : 0 < h) (hm : 0 < m)
  (hphi : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsRightLittleOAtZero phi) :
  (∃ delta > 0, ∀ (epsilon : ℝ), 0 < epsilon → epsilon < delta → h * phi epsilon - m * epsilon < 0) ∧
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueScalarMinimizerOn (fun x => x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1)
      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.oneFollowerQuadraticCost h m)
      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.clippedQuadraticOptimizer h m)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b5_complete_exponent_boundary {pSub pSup : ℝ} (hpSub0 : 0 < pSub)
  (hpSub1 : pSub < 1) (hpSup1 : 1 < pSup) :
  (∀ (t : ℝ),
      0 ≤ t →
        t ≤ 1 →
          ((∀ s ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentFaceObjective pSub t ≤
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentFaceObjective pSub s) ↔
            t = 0 ∨ t = 1)) ∧
    (∀ (t : ℝ), PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentFaceObjective 1 t = 0) ∧
      (∀ (a b : ℝ), 0 ≤ a → 0 ≤ b → (a + b) ^ pSub ≤ a ^ pSub + b ^ pSub) ∧
        (∀ (a b : ℝ), (a + b) ^ 1 = a ^ 1 + b ^ 1) ∧ ∀ (a b : ℝ), 0 ≤ a → 0 ≤ b → a ^ pSup + b ^ pSup ≤ (a + b) ^ pSup
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b5_endpoints_01 :
  (∀ t ∈ {0, 1}, t = 0 ∨ t = 1) ∧
    ∀ t ∈ {0, 1 / 4, 1 / 2, 3 / 4, 1}, PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentFaceObjective 1 t = 0
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b5_full_path_exponent_boundary :
  (∀ (N : ℕ),
      0 < N →
        ∀ (h m p : ℝ),
          0 < h →
            0 < m →
              0 < p →
                p < 1 →
                  m * ↑N < h →
                    ∀ (d : Fin N → ℝ), PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsPowerPathMinimizer h m p d ↔ d = 0) ∧
    (∀ (N : ℕ) (hN : 0 < N) (h m p : ℝ),
        0 < h →
          0 < m →
            0 < p →
              p < 1 →
                h = m * ↑N →
                  ∀ (d : Fin N → ℝ),
                    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsPowerPathMinimizer h m p d ↔
                      d = 0 ∨ d = PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentBoundaryFirstSegment hN 1) ∧
      (∀ (N : ℕ) (hN : 0 < N) (h m p : ℝ),
          0 < h →
            0 < m →
              0 < p →
                p < 1 →
                  h < m * ↑N →
                    ∀ (d : Fin N → ℝ),
                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsPowerPathMinimizer h m p d ↔
                        d = PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentBoundaryFirstSegment hN 1) ∧
        (∀ (N : ℕ),
            0 < N →
              ∀ (h m : ℝ),
                0 < m →
                  m * ↑N < h →
                    ∀ (d : Fin N → ℝ), PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsPowerPathMinimizer h m 1 d ↔ d = 0) ∧
          (∀ (N : ℕ) (hN : 0 < N) (h m : ℝ),
              0 < m →
                h = m * ↑N →
                  ∀ (d : Fin N → ℝ),
                    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsPowerPathMinimizer h m 1 d ↔
                      ∃ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, d = PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentBoundaryFirstSegment hN t) ∧
            (∀ (N : ℕ) (hN : 0 < N) (h m : ℝ),
                0 < m →
                  h < m * ↑N →
                    ∀ (d : Fin N → ℝ),
                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsPowerPathMinimizer h m 1 d ↔
                        d = PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentBoundaryFirstSegment hN 1) ∧
              ∀ (N : ℕ) (p : ℝ),
                1 < p → ∀ (d : Fin N → ℝ), (∀ (i : Fin N), 0 ≤ d i) → (∑ i, d i) ^ p ≤ ↑N ^ (p - 1) * ∑ i, d i ^ p
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b5_repair_02 : 4 * (1 / 4) ^ 2 < 1 ^ 2 ∧ 4 * (1 / 4) = 1 ∧ 4 * (1 / 2) > 1
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b6_allBack_unique_iff {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) {h m delta p : ℝ} (hh : 0 < h)
  (hm : 0 < m) (hdelta : 0 < delta) (hdeltaOne : delta ≤ 1) (hp : 1 ≤ p) :
  (∀ (count : Fin N → ℕ),
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeAdmissible delta count →
        count ≠ 0 →
          0 <
            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeScore
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.directionalLatticeCoefficient h delta p) m p
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPathWeight N) count) ↔
    m * ↑N < PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.directionalLatticeCoefficient h delta p
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b6_allBack_weak_iff {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) {h m delta p : ℝ} (hh : 0 < h)
  (hm : 0 < m) (hdelta : 0 < delta) (hdeltaOne : delta ≤ 1) (hp : 1 ≤ p) :
  (∀ (count : Fin N → ℕ),
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeAdmissible delta count →
        0 ≤
          PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeScore
            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.directionalLatticeCoefficient h delta p) m p
            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPathWeight N) count) ↔
    m * ↑N ≤ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.directionalLatticeCoefficient h delta p
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b6_linear_boundary_ties {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) {h m delta : ℝ} (hm : 0 < m)
  (hboundary : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.directionalLatticeCoefficient h delta 1 = m * ↑N) (count : Fin N → ℕ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeScore
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.directionalLatticeCoefficient h delta 1) m 1
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPathWeight N) count =
      0 ↔
    ∀ (i : Fin N), i ≠ ⟨0, hN⟩ → count i = 0
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b6_oneStep_difference {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (h m delta p : ℝ) (hp : 1 ≤ p) :
  delta *
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeScore
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.directionalLatticeCoefficient h delta p) m p
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPathWeight N)
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.singleLatticeCount ⟨0, hN⟩) =
    delta * (h * delta ^ (p - 1) - m * ↑N)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b6_superlinear_boundary_ties {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) {h m delta p : ℝ} (hm : 0 < m)
  (hp : 1 < p) (hboundary : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.directionalLatticeCoefficient h delta p = m * ↑N)
  (count : Fin N → ℕ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeScore
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.directionalLatticeCoefficient h delta p) m p
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticPathWeight N) count =
      0 ↔
    count = 0 ∨ count = PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.singleLatticeCount ⟨0, hN⟩
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b7_common_scale_preorder (penalty : ℝ → ℝ) {lambda : ℝ} (hlambda : 0 < lambda)
  (h m : ℝ) (x y : List ℝ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony penalty (lambda * h) (lambda * m) x ≤
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony penalty (lambda * h) (lambda * m) y ↔
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony penalty h m x ≤
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony penalty h m y
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b7_complete_general_power_persistence {p : ℝ} (hp : 1 < p) (K : ℝ → ℕ)
  (hphase : ∀ (rho : ℝ), 0 < rho → PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerRatioPhaseCell p rho (K rho)) :
  (∀ (penalty : ℝ → ℝ) (lambda h m : ℝ),
      0 < lambda →
        ∀ (x y : List ℝ),
          PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony penalty (lambda * h) (lambda * m) x ≤
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony penalty (lambda * h) (lambda * m) y ↔
            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony penalty h m x ≤
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony penalty h m y) ∧
    (∀ (h m : ℝ) (L : ℕ),
        0 < h →
          0 < m →
            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell h m p L ↔
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerRatioPhaseCell p (h / m) L)) ∧
      (∀ (rho : ℝ) (L : ℕ),
          0 < rho → PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerRatioPhaseCell p rho L → L = K rho) ∧
        MonotoneOn K (Set.Ioi 0) ∧
          Filter.Tendsto (fun rho => ↑(K rho) / rho ^ (1 / p)) Filter.atTop (nhds (p * (p - 1) ^ (-(p - 1) / p)))

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.chg_b9_complete_optimizer_phase_profile {h m p : ℝ} {K : ℕ}
  (hcell : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell h m p K) :
  ∃ eta tau,
    0 ≤ eta ∧
      eta < m ∧
        tau = eta / m ∧
          tau ∈ Set.Ico 0 1 ∧
            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass h m p eta K = 1 ∧
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.shiftedPowerSum (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p)
                    tau K =
                  (p * (h / m)) ^ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p ∧
                (∀ other ∈ Set.Ico 0 1,
                    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.shiftedPowerSum
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) other K =
                        (p * (h / m)) ^ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p →
                      other = tau) ∧
                  (∀ (i : ℕ),
                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p eta K) i =
                        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tau K i) ∧
                    (∀ (i j : ℕ),
                        i < j →
                          j < K →
                            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedProfileLoss
                                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tau K i ^
                                  (p - 1) -
                                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedProfileLoss
                                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tau K j ^
                                  (p - 1) =
                              ↑(j - i) / (p * (h / m))) ∧
                      ∀ (i : ℕ),
                        i + 1 < K →
                          0 <
                            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile
                                  (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tau K i -
                                2 *
                                  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile
                                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tau K (i + 1) +
                              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile
                                (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tau K (i + 2)

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exactUniformSimplexLatticeMinimizer_spec {N : ℕ} (objective : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ)
  (hcontinuous : Continuous objective) (n : ℕ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exactUniformSimplexLatticeMinimizer objective hcontinuous n ∈
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformSimplexLattice n ∧
    ∀ y ∈ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformSimplexLattice n,
      objective (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exactUniformSimplexLatticeMinimizer objective hcontinuous n) ≤
        objective y

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.generalPowerFirstZeroCell_least_stablePositive {h m p : ℝ} {K : ℕ}
  (hcell : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell h m p K) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerStablePositiveHorizon h m p K ∧
    ∀ (J : ℕ), 0 < J → J < K → ¬PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerStablePositiveHorizon h m p J
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.generalPowerFirstZeroCell_minimal_extension_package {h m p : ℝ} {K : ℕ}
  (hcell : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell h m p K) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerStablePositiveHorizon h m p K ∧
    (∀ (J : ℕ), 0 < J → J < K → ¬PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerStablePositiveHorizon h m p J) ∧
      ∃ eta,
        0 ≤ eta ∧
          eta < m ∧
            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass h m p eta K = 1 ∧
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective h m p
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight K))
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p eta K) ∧
                ∀ (R : ℕ),
                  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective h m p
                        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight (K + R)))
                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p (eta + m * ↑R) (K + R)) ∧
                    ∀ (i : ℕ),
                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p (eta + m * ↑R) (K + R)) i =
                        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p eta K) i

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.generalPower_all_horizon_finite_persistence {h m p : ℝ} {K : ℕ}
  (hcell : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell h m p K) :
  ∃ eta,
    0 ≤ eta ∧
      eta < m ∧
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass h m p eta K = 1 ∧
          PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective h m p
                (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight K))
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p eta K) ∧
            (∀ (i : ℕ),
                0 <
                    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p eta K) i ↔
                  i < K) ∧
              ∀ (R : ℕ),
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective h m p
                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight (K + R)))
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p (eta + m * ↑R) (K + R)) ∧
                  (∀ (i : Fin K),
                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p (eta + m * ↑R) (K + R)
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathOldIndex R i) =
                        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p eta K i) ∧
                    ∀ (j : Fin R),
                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p (eta + m * ↑R) (K + R)
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathNewIndex K j) =
                        0

            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p eta K)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.generalPower_boundary_optimizer_profile_global_mono_ratio {p rhoOne rhoTwo : ℝ}
  {K L : ℕ} (hp : 1 < p) (hrhoOne : 0 < rhoOne) (hrhoTwo : 0 < rhoTwo) (hrho : rhoOne ≤ rhoTwo)
  (hcellOne : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell rhoOne 1 p K)
  (hcellTwo : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerFirstZeroCell rhoTwo 1 p L) :
  ∃ etaOne etaTwo,
    0 ≤ etaOne ∧
      etaOne < 1 ∧
        0 ≤ etaTwo ∧
          etaTwo < 1 ∧
            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass rhoOne 1 p etaOne K = 1 ∧
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass rhoTwo 1 p etaTwo L = 1 ∧
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoOne 1 p
                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight K))
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease rhoOne 1 p etaOne K) ∧
                  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoTwo 1 p
                        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight L))
                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease rhoTwo 1 p etaTwo L) ∧
                    ∀ (i : ℕ),
                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease rhoOne 1 p etaOne K) i ≤
                        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
                          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease rhoTwo 1 p etaTwo L) i

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.generalPower_monotone_simplex_strict_package.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι]
  {h m p : ℝ} (weight d u : ι → ℝ) (hh : 0 < h) (hm : 0 < m) (hp : 1 < p) (hd : ∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ d i)
  (hu : ∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ u i) :
  (∀ (profile : List ℝ),
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.realPowerPenalty p) h m
          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.runningMinimum profile) ≤
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.realPowerPenalty p) h m
          profile) ∧
    (∀ (profile : List ℝ),
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.runningMinimum profile ≠ profile →
          PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.realPowerPenalty p) h m
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.runningMinimum profile) <
            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.realPowerPenalty p) h m
              profile) ∧
      ((∀ (profile : List ℝ),
            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.AdmissibleProfileFrom 1 profile →
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplexFrom 1
                (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.decreasesFrom 1 profile)) ∧
          (∀ (decreases : List ℝ),
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplexFrom 1 decreases →
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.AdmissibleProfileFrom 1
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.profileFromDecreases 1 decreases)) ∧
            (∀ (profile : List ℝ),
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.profileFromDecreases 1
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.decreasesFrom 1 profile) =
                  profile) ∧
              ∀ (decreases : List ℝ),
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.decreasesFrom 1
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.profileFromDecreases 1 decreases) =
                  decreases) ∧
        (d ≠ u →
          ∑ i, PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerGradient h m p weight u i * (d i - u i) <
            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective h m p weight d -
              PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective h m p weight u)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.generalPower_phase_index_div_rpow_tendsto_canonical {p : ℝ} (hp : 1 < p) (K : ℝ → ℕ)

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseDecrease_uniform_scaling {p : ℝ} (hp : 1 < p) (epsilon : ℝ) :
  0 < epsilon →
    ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
      ∀ (tau : ℝ),
        0 ≤ tau →
          tau < 1 →
            ∀ (i : ℕ),
              1 ≤ i →
                i ≤ K →
                  |↑K *
                          PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseDecrease
                            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tau K (K - i) -
                        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.scalingDensity p (↑i / ↑K)| <
                    epsilon
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile_global_mono_ratio {p rhoOne rhoTwo tauOne tauTwo : ℝ}
  {K L i : ℕ} (hp : 1 < p) (hrhoOne : 0 < rhoOne) (_hrhoTwo : 0 < rhoTwo) (hrho : rhoOne ≤ rhoTwo)
  (hcellOne : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerRatioPhaseCell p rhoOne K)
  (hcellTwo : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.GeneralPowerRatioPhaseCell p rhoTwo L) (htauOne : tauOne ∈ Set.Ico 0 1)

    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.shiftedPowerSum (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tauOne

      (p * rhoOne) ^ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p)
  (hphaseTwo :
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.shiftedPowerSum (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tauTwo

  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p)
      tauOne K i ≤
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p)
      tauTwo L i

    (nhdsWithin 1 (Set.Iio 1)) (nhds (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile q 0 K i))
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile_strict_secondDifference_pos {q tau : ℝ} {K i : ℕ} (hq : 0 < q)
  (htau : tau < 1) (hden : 0 < PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.shiftedPowerSum q tau K) (hi : i + 1 < K) :
  0 <
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile q tau K i -
        2 * PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile q tau K (i + 1) +
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile q tau K (i + 2)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile_sub_succ {q tau : ℝ} {K i : ℕ} (hi : i < K) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile q tau K i -
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile q tau K (i + 1) =
    (↑(K - i) - tau) ^ q / PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.shiftedPowerSum q tau K
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile_uniform_scaling {p : ℝ} (hp : 1 < p) (epsilon : ℝ) :
  0 < epsilon →
    ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
      ∀ (tau : ℝ),
        0 ≤ tau →
          tau < 1 →
            ∀ i ≤ K,
              |PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile
                      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tau K i -
                    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.scalingProfile p (↑i / ↑K)| <
                epsilon
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedProfileLoss_powered_gap {p rho tau : ℝ} {K i j : ℕ} (hp : 1 < p)
  (hrho : 0 < rho) (_htau0 : 0 ≤ tau) (htau1 : tau < 1) (hij : i < j) (hjK : j < K)
  (hphase : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.shiftedPowerSum (1 / (p - 1)) tau K = (p * rho) ^ (1 / (p - 1))) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedProfileLoss (1 / (p - 1)) tau K i ^ (p - 1) -
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedProfileLoss (1 / (p - 1)) tau K j ^ (p - 1) =
    ↑(j - i) / (p * rho)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony_common_scale (penalty : ℝ → ℝ) (lambda h m : ℝ) (profile : List ℝ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony penalty (lambda * h) (lambda * m) profile =
    lambda * PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.pathHarmony penalty h m profile
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.phaseTwoNormalizedFollower_sharp_bounds {rho p eta : ℝ} (hrho : 0 < rho) (hp : 2 ≤ p)
  (hetaZero : 0 ≤ eta) (hetaOne : eta < 1)
  (hphase :
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.shiftedPowerSum (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) eta 2 =
      (p * rho) ^ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.hardExponentFollower rho p /
        (1 + PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) ≤
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.phaseTwoNormalizedFollower p eta ∧
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.phaseTwoNormalizedFollower p eta ≤
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.hardExponentFollower rho p
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.phaseTwo_actualFollower_div_scale_tendsto_one {rho : ℝ} (hrho : 0 < rho) (eta : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hadmitted :
    ∀ᶠ (p : ℝ) in Filter.atTop,
      1 < p ∧ 0 ≤ eta p ∧ eta p < 1 ∧ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass rho 1 p (eta p) 2 = 1) :
  Filter.Tendsto
    (fun p =>
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease rho 1 p (eta p) 2) 1 /
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.hardExponentScale rho p)
    Filter.atTop (nhds 1)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.phaseTwo_actualFollower_tendsto_zero {rho : ℝ} (hrho : 0 < rho) (eta : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hadmitted :
    ∀ᶠ (p : ℝ) in Filter.atTop,
      1 < p ∧ 0 ≤ eta p ∧ eta p < 1 ∧ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass rho 1 p (eta p) 2 = 1) :

    (fun p =>
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease rho 1 p (eta p) 2) 1)
    Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.positivePower_ratio_tendsto_zero {q b : ℝ} {denominator : ℝ → ℝ} (hq : 0 < q)
  (hb : 0 < b) (hden : Filter.Tendsto denominator (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0)) (nhds b)) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun t => t ^ q / denominator t) (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0)) (nhds 0)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerKKTDecrease_unique_minimizer.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι] {h m p eta : ℝ}
  (weight : ι → ℝ) (hh : 0 < h) (hp : 1 < p) (heta : 0 ≤ eta)

    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective h m p weight)
    (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerKKTDecrease h m p eta weight)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerObjective_strict_supporting_hyperplane.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι]
  {h m p : ℝ} (weight d u : ι → ℝ) (hh : 0 < h) (hp : 1 < p) (hd : ∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ d i) (hu : ∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ u i)
  (hne : d ≠ u) :

    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective h m p weight d -
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective h m p weight u
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease_extension_new_zero {h m p eta : ℝ} {K R : ℕ} (_hh : 0 < h)
  (hm : 0 < m) (hp : 1 < p) (heta : 0 ≤ eta) (j : Fin R) :

      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathNewIndex K j) =
    0
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease_extension_old {h m p eta : ℝ} {K : ℕ} (R : ℕ) (i : Fin K) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p (eta + m * ↑R) (K + R)
      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathOldIndex R i) =
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p eta K i
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass_one_iff_phaseEquation {h m p eta : ℝ} {K : ℕ} (hh : 0 < h) (hm : 0 < m)
  (hp : 1 < p) (heta : eta < m) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass h m p eta K = 1 ↔
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.shiftedPowerSum (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) (eta / m)
        K =
      (p * (h / m)) ^ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMinimumValue_concave_all_phases {rhoOne rhoTwo theta p : ℝ} (N : ℕ)
  (hrhoOne : 0 < rhoOne) (hrhoTwo : 0 < rhoTwo) (hp : 1 < p) (_hN : 0 < N) (hthetaZero : 0 ≤ theta)
  (hthetaOne : theta ≤ 1) :
  ∃ winnerOne winnerTwo winnerMiddle,
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoOne 1 p
          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N))
        winnerOne ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoTwo 1 p
            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N))
          winnerTwo ∧
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
            (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective (theta * rhoOne + (1 - theta) * rhoTwo) 1 p
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N))
            winnerMiddle ∧
          theta *
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoOne 1 p
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N) winnerOne +
              (1 - theta) *
                PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective rhoTwo 1 p
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N) winnerTwo ≤
            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective (theta * rhoOne + (1 - theta) * rhoTwo) 1 p
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N) winnerMiddle
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases_eq_normalizedPhaseProfile {h m p eta : ℝ} {K i : ℕ}
  (hh : 0 < h) (hm : 0 < m) (hp : 1 < p) (heta : eta < m)
  (hmass : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathMass h m p eta K = 1) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerProfileFromDecreases
      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease h m p eta K) i =
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.normalizedPhaseProfile (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p)
      (eta / m) K i

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.poweredGapFamilyAdmission_consecutive_triple_identifies {p rho : ℝ} {decrease : ℕ → ℝ}
  {K t : ℕ} (hadmit : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.PoweredGapFamilyAdmission p rho decrease K) (htK : t + 2 < K)
  (hc : 0 < decrease (t + 2)) (hcb : decrease (t + 2) < decrease (t + 1)) (hba : decrease (t + 1) < decrease t)
  (hLogConcave : decrease t * decrease (t + 2) < decrease (t + 1) ^ 2) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.TripleExponentRatioSystem (decrease t) (decrease (t + 1)) (decrease (t + 2)) p rho ∧
    ∀ (parameter : ℝ × ℝ),
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.TripleExponentRatioSystem (decrease t) (decrease (t + 1)) (decrease (t + 2))
          parameter.1 parameter.2 →
        parameter = (p, rho)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powered_gap_pair_consistency {p rho gap₁ gap₂ indexGap₁ indexGap₂ : ℝ} (hp : 0 < p)
  (hrho : 0 < rho) (hi₁ : 0 < indexGap₁) (hi₂ : 0 < indexGap₂) (hg₁ : gap₁ = indexGap₁ / (p * rho))
  (hg₂ : gap₂ = indexGap₂ / (p * rho)) : indexGap₁ / (p * gap₁) = indexGap₂ / (p * gap₂)

    0 < rho ∧ 0 < K ∧ ↑(K - 1) * ↑K / 4 < rho ∧ rho ≤ ↑K * ↑(K + 1) / 4

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadratic_minimizer_is_metric_projection.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι] {h m : ℝ}
  (hh : 0 < h) (weight u : ι → ℝ)
  (hu :
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticReducedObjective h m weight) u) :
  have center := WithLp.toLp 2 fun i => m * weight i / (2 * h);
  have projected := WithLp.toLp 2 u;
  have carrier := {z | PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex fun i => z.ofLp i};
  ‖center - projected‖ = ⨅ z, ‖center - ↑z‖
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadratic_phase_four_iff_ratio_cell {h m : ℝ} (hh : 0 < h) (hm : 0 < m) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.QuadraticPhaseCell h m 4 ↔ 3 < h / m ∧ h / m ≤ 5

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadratic_ratio_minimizers_nonexpansive.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι]
  (weight u₁ u₂ : ι → ℝ) {rho₁ rho₂ : ℝ} (hrho₁ : 0 < rho₁) (hrho₂ : 0 < rho₂)
  (hu₁ :
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticReducedObjective rho₁ 1 weight) u₁)
  (hu₂ :
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
      (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadraticReducedObjective rho₂ 1 weight) u₂) :
  ‖WithLp.toLp 2 u₁ - WithLp.toLp 2 u₂‖ ≤
    ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 fun i => weight i / (2 * rho₁)) - WithLp.toLp 2 fun i => weight i / (2 * rho₂)‖
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.quadratic_unconstrained_ratio_distance.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι]
  (weight : ι → ℝ) {rho₁ rho₂ : ℝ} (hrho₁ : rho₁ ≠ 0) (hrho₂ : rho₂ ≠ 0) :
  ∑ i, (weight i / (2 * rho₁) - weight i / (2 * rho₂)) ^ 2 = (∑ i, weight i ^ 2) * (1 / rho₁ - 1 / rho₂) ^ 2 / 4
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.rpow_strict_tangent {p u d : ℝ} (hp : 1 < p) (hu : 0 ≤ u) (hd : 0 ≤ d) :
  u ^ p + p * u ^ (p - 1) * (d - u) ≤ d ^ p ∧ (u ^ p + p * u ^ (p - 1) * (d - u) = d ^ p → d = u)

  ∫ (u : ℝ) in 0..1, PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.scalingDensity p u = 1

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.shiftedPowerSum_uniform_integral_scaling {p : ℝ} (hp : 1 < p) (epsilon : ℝ) :
  0 < epsilon →
    ∀ᶠ (K : ℕ) in Filter.atTop,
      ∀ (tau : ℝ),
        0 ≤ tau →
          tau < 1 →
            ∀ n ≤ K,
              |PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.shiftedPowerSum
                        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerShiftExponent p) tau n /
                      ↑K ^ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.scalingExponent p -
                    (↑n / ↑K) ^ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.scalingExponent p /
                      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.scalingExponent p| <
                epsilon
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.singular_fullVector_joint_path {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) {c : ℝ} (hc : -1 < c) :

      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerReducedObjective (↑N * (1 + c / x)) 1 (1 + 1 / x)
          (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathWeight N))
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease (↑N * (1 + c / x)) 1 (1 + 1 / x) 0 N)) ∧

        j ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.singularFirstIndex hN →

            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.singularActivityAt
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease (↑N * (1 + c / x)) 1 (1 + 1 / x) 0 N) i)
          Filter.atTop (nhds (1 - Real.exp (-(1 + c))))

      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueMinimizerOn PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex

    (∀ (j : Fin N),
        j ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.singularFirstIndex hN →
          Filter.Tendsto (fun x => PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease (↑N) 1 (1 + 1 / x) 0 N j)
            Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) ∧
      ∀ (i : Fin N),
        Filter.Tendsto
          (fun x =>
            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.singularActivityAt
              (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.powerPathDecrease (↑N) 1 (1 + 1 / x) 0 N) i)
          Filter.atTop (nhds (1 - Real.exp (-1)))
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.singular_selected_set_ne_linear_tie_face : {0, Real.exp (-1), 1} ≠ Set.Icc 0 1
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.solidSimplex_euclidean_convex.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι] :
  Convex ℝ {z | PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex fun i => z.ofLp i}
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.sublinear_concentrated_repair_le_split {p a b : ℝ} (hp0 : 0 < p) (hp1 : p < 1)
  (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hb : 0 ≤ b) : (a + b) ^ p ≤ a ^ p + b ^ p
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.sublinear_exponentFace_minimizer_iff_endpoint {p t : ℝ} (hp0 : 0 < p) (hp1 : p < 1)
  (ht0 : 0 ≤ t) (ht1 : t ≤ 1) :
  (∀ s ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentFaceObjective p t ≤
        PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentFaceObjective p s) ↔
    t = 0 ∨ t = 1
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.sublinear_powerPath_minimizer_above_iff {N : ℕ} (_hN : 0 < N) {h m p : ℝ} (hh : 0 < h)
  (hm : 0 < m) (hp0 : 0 < p) (hp1 : p < 1) (habove : m * ↑N < h) (d : Fin N → ℝ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsPowerPathMinimizer h m p d ↔ d = 0
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.sublinear_powerPath_minimizer_below_iff {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) {h m p : ℝ} (hh : 0 < h)
  (hm : 0 < m) (hp0 : 0 < p) (hp1 : p < 1) (hbelow : h < m * ↑N) (d : Fin N → ℝ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsPowerPathMinimizer h m p d ↔
    d = PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentBoundaryFirstSegment hN 1
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.sublinear_powerPath_minimizer_equal_iff {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) {h m p : ℝ} (hh : 0 < h)
  (hm : 0 < m) (hp0 : 0 < p) (hp1 : p < 1) (hequal : h = m * ↑N) (d : Fin N → ℝ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsPowerPathMinimizer h m p d ↔
    d = 0 ∨ d = PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.exponentBoundaryFirstSegment hN 1
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.superlinear_finite_equal_distribution_lowerBound {N : ℕ} {p : ℝ} (hp : 1 < p)
  (d : Fin N → ℝ) (hd : ∀ (i : Fin N), 0 ≤ d i) : (∑ i, d i) ^ p ≤ ↑N ^ (p - 1) * ∑ i, d i ^ p
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.superlinear_split_repair_le_concentrated {p a b : ℝ} (hp1 : 1 < p) (ha : 0 ≤ a)
  (hb : 0 ≤ b) : a ^ p + b ^ p ≤ (a + b) ^ p
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.supportBirthCoordinateCoefficients_pos {p q : ℝ} (hp : 0 < p) (hq : 1 < q) {k : ℕ}
  (hk : 0 < k) :
  0 < PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.supportBirthSubquadraticCoordinateCoefficient p q k ∧
    0 < PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.supportBirthQuadraticCoordinateCoefficient p k ∧
      ∀ (r : ℝ),
        0 < r → r < 1 → 0 < PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.supportBirthSuperquadraticCoordinateCoefficient p r k
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.supportBirthCoordinate_div_ratioExcess_rpow_tendsto_subquadratic {p q : ℝ}
  (hp : 0 < p) (hq : 1 < q) {k : ℕ} (hk : 0 < k) :
  Filter.Tendsto
    (fun t =>
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.supportBirthCoordinate q k t /
        (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.supportBirthRatio p q k t -
            PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.supportBirthRatio p q k 0) ^
          q)
    (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0))
    (nhds (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.supportBirthSubquadraticCoordinateCoefficient p q k))

    (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0))
    (nhds (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.supportBirthSuperquadraticCoordinateCoefficient p q k))

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.supportBirthQuadraticCoordinateCoefficient_closedForm {k : ℕ} (hk : 0 < k) :

PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.uniformLattice_allBack_unique_iff.{u_1} {index : Type u_1} [Fintype index]
  [DecidableEq index] {delta edgeCoefficient markedness p horizon : ℝ} (hdelta : 0 < delta) (hdeltaOne : delta ≤ 1)
  (hmarkedness : 0 < markedness) (hhorizon : 0 ≤ horizon) (hp : 1 ≤ p) (weight : index → ℝ) (first : index)
  (hweight : ∀ (i : index), weight i ≤ horizon) (hfirst : weight first = horizon) :
  (∀ (count : index → ℕ),
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeAdmissible delta count →
        count ≠ 0 →
          0 < PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeScore edgeCoefficient markedness p weight count) ↔
    markedness * horizon < edgeCoefficient
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.uniformLattice_allBack_weak_iff.{u_1} {index : Type u_1} [Fintype index]
  [DecidableEq index] {delta edgeCoefficient markedness p horizon : ℝ} (hdelta : 0 < delta) (hdeltaOne : delta ≤ 1)
  (hmarkedness : 0 < markedness) (hhorizon : 0 ≤ horizon) (hp : 1 ≤ p) (weight : index → ℝ) (first : index)
  (hweight : ∀ (i : index), weight i ≤ horizon) (hfirst : weight first = horizon) :
  (∀ (count : index → ℕ),
      PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeAdmissible delta count →
        0 ≤ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeScore edgeCoefficient markedness p weight count) ↔
    markedness * horizon ≤ edgeCoefficient
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.uniformLattice_linear_boundary_ties.{u_1} {index : Type u_1} [Fintype index]
  [DecidableEq index] {edgeCoefficient markedness horizon : ℝ} (hmarkedness : 0 < markedness) (weight : index → ℝ)
  (first : index) (hweight : ∀ (i : index), weight i ≤ horizon) (hfirst : weight first = horizon)
  (hunique : ∀ (i : index), weight i = horizon → i = first) (hboundary : edgeCoefficient = markedness * horizon)
  (count : index → ℕ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeScore edgeCoefficient markedness 1 weight count = 0 ↔
    ∀ (i : index), i ≠ first → count i = 0
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.uniformLattice_superlinear_boundary_ties.{u_1} {index : Type u_1} [Fintype index]
  [DecidableEq index] {edgeCoefficient markedness p horizon : ℝ} (hmarkedness : 0 < markedness) (hhorizon : 0 < horizon)
  (hp : 1 < p) (weight : index → ℝ) (first : index) (hweight : ∀ (i : index), weight i ≤ horizon)
  (hfirst : weight first = horizon) (hunique : ∀ (i : index), weight i = horizon → i = first)
  (hboundary : edgeCoefficient = markedness * horizon) (count : index → ℕ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformLatticeScore edgeCoefficient markedness p weight count = 0 ↔
    count = 0 ∨ count = PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.singleLatticeCount first
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.uniformSimplexFloorApprox_coordinate_tendsto {N : ℕ} (u : Fin N → ℝ)
  (hu : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex u) (i : Fin N) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun n => PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.uniformSimplexFloorApprox u n i) Filter.atTop (nhds (u i))
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.uniformSimplexFloorApprox_mem {N : ℕ} (u : Fin N → ℝ)
  (hu : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex u) (n : ℕ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.uniformSimplexFloorApprox u n ∈
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformSimplexLattice n
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.uniformSimplexFloorApprox_tendsto {N : ℕ} (u : Fin N → ℝ)
  (hu : PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex u) :
  Filter.Tendsto (PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.uniformSimplexFloorApprox u) Filter.atTop (nhds u)
PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.uniformSimplexLattice_minimizers_converge {N : ℕ} {objective : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ}
  {optimizer : Fin N → ℝ} {latticeMinimizer : ℕ → Fin N → ℝ} (hcontinuous : Continuous objective)
  (hunique :
    PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.IsUniqueCompactMinimizerOn {d | PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.SolidSimplex d}
      objective optimizer)
  (hlattice : ∀ (n : ℕ), latticeMinimizer n ∈ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformSimplexLattice n)
  (hoptimal :
    ∀ (n : ℕ),
      ∀ d ∈ PhonologicalCalculus.ContinuousHG.UniformSimplexLattice n, objective (latticeMinimizer n) ≤ objective d) :
  Filter.Tendsto latticeMinimizer Filter.atTop (nhds optimizer)

PhonologicalCalculus.Equivariant.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {A : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {Y : Type u_3} (sourceAction : A → X → X)
  (targetAction : A → Y → Y) (f : X → Y) : Prop
PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.RawFiniteContract.form_eq_none_iff_incomplete
  {Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer Evaluator Layer Presentation Query : Type}
  (raw :
    PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.RawFiniteContract Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer Evaluator Layer Presentation
      Query) :
  raw.form = none ↔ raw.formationChecklist.complete = false
PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.RawFiniteContract.form_isSome_eq_complete
  {Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer Evaluator Layer Presentation Query : Type}
  (raw :
    PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.RawFiniteContract Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer Evaluator Layer Presentation
      Query) :
  raw.form.isSome = raw.formationChecklist.complete
PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.RawFiniteContract.qualifiedFiniteContractDecision
  {Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer Evaluator Layer Presentation Query : Type} [Fintype Row] [DecidableEq Row]
  [DecidableEq TargetAnswer]
  (raw :
    PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.RawFiniteContract Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer Evaluator Layer Presentation
      Query) :
  raw.form.isSome = raw.formationChecklist.complete ∧
    (raw.form = none → raw.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.notEvaluated) ∧
      (raw.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.notEvaluated ↔
          raw.form = none ∨ ∃ contract, raw.form = some contract ∧ ¬contract.toRequest.eligible) ∧
        (raw.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.conservative ↔
            ∃ contract,
              raw.form = some contract ∧
                contract.toRequest.eligible ∧
                  ∀ row ∈ contract.matchedRows,
                    contract.transport (contract.sourceAnswer row) = contract.targetAnswer row) ∧
          (raw.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.nonconservative ↔
              ∃ contract,
                raw.form = some contract ∧
                  contract.toRequest.eligible ∧
                    ∃ row ∈ contract.matchedRows,
                      contract.transport (contract.sourceAnswer row) ≠ contract.targetAnswer row) ∧
            (raw.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.notEvaluated ∨
              raw.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.conservative ∨
                raw.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.nonconservative)
PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.TypedFiniteContract.eligible_iff_explicit_admission
  {Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer Evaluator Layer Presentation Query : Type} [Fintype Row] [DecidableEq Row]
  (contract :
    PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.TypedFiniteContract Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer Evaluator Layer Presentation
      Query) :
  contract.toRequest.eligible ↔ contract.admission.complete = true ∧ contract.matchedRows.Nonempty
PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.TypedFiniteContract.mem_matchedRows_iff
  {Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer Evaluator Layer Presentation Query : Type} [Fintype Row] [DecidableEq Row]
  (contract :
    PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.TypedFiniteContract Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer Evaluator Layer Presentation
      Query)
  (row : Row) : row ∈ contract.matchedRows ↔ contract.licensedRow row = true
PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.calc_f1_least_witness_03.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Row : Type u_1} {SourceAnswer : Type u_2}
  {TargetAnswer : Type u_3} [LinearOrder Row] [DecidableEq TargetAnswer]
  (r : PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer)
  (hVerdict : r.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.nonconservative) :
  ∃ (h : r.mismatchSet.Nonempty),
    r.selectedWitness = some (r.mismatchSet.min' h) ∧
      r.mismatchSet.min' h ∈ r.mismatchSet ∧ ∀ row ∈ r.mismatchSet, r.mismatchSet.min' h ≤ row
PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.calc_f1_progress_02.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Row : Type u_1} {SourceAnswer : Type u_2}
  {TargetAnswer : Type u_3} [DecidableEq Row] [DecidableEq TargetAnswer]
  (r : PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer) :
  r.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.conservative ∨
    r.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.nonconservative ∨ r.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.notEvaluated

PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.calc_f1_witness_03.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Row : Type u_1} {SourceAnswer : Type u_2}
  {TargetAnswer : Type u_3} [DecidableEq Row] [DecidableEq TargetAnswer]
  (r : PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer)
  (hVerdict : r.evaluate = PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.nonconservative) :
  r.mismatchSet.Nonempty ∧
    ∀ (row : Row), row ∈ r.mismatchSet ↔ row ∈ r.rows ∧ r.transport (r.sourceAnswer row) ≠ r.targetAnswer row

PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest.mem_mismatchSet_iff.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Row : Type u_1} {SourceAnswer : Type u_2}
  {TargetAnswer : Type u_3} [DecidableEq Row] [DecidableEq TargetAnswer]
  (r : PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRequest Row SourceAnswer TargetAnswer) (row : Row) :
  row ∈ r.mismatchSet ↔ row ∈ r.rows ∧ r.transport (r.sourceAnswer row) ≠ r.targetAnswer row

      PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.nonconservative ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.Verdict.notEvaluated

PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.flux_d1_completePathWinnerBridge.{u_1, u_2} :
  (∀ {I : Type u_1} {Candidate : Type u_2} {F : ℝ → ℝ}
      (status : Candidate → I → PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.BoxSiteStatus) (leftFlux rightFlux : Candidate → I → ℝ)
      (lambda : ℝ) (baselineWinner transformedWinner : Candidate → Prop),
      PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ExactWinnerKKT baselineWinner
          (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.baselinePathKKT status leftFlux rightFlux lambda) →
        PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ExactWinnerKKT transformedWinner
            (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.transformedPathKKT F status leftFlux rightFlux lambda) →
          StrictMono F → PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ShiftEquivariant F lambda → baselineWinner = transformedWinner) ∧
    ∀ (F : ℝ → ℝ) (lambda : ℝ) (baselineWinner transformedWinner : ℝ → Unit → Prop),
      (∀ (y : ℝ),
          PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ExactWinnerKKT (baselineWinner y)
            (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.baselineFreeTwoEdgeProbeKKT lambda y)) →
        (∀ (y : ℝ),
            PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ExactWinnerKKT (transformedWinner y)
              (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.transformedFreeTwoEdgeProbeKKT F lambda y)) →
          (∀ (y : ℝ), baselineWinner y = transformedWinner y) → PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ShiftEquivariant F lambda

PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.flux_d2_completeWinnerRigidity :
  (∀ (F : ℝ → ℝ) (firstLoad secondLoad : ℝ)
      (baselineWinnerFirst transformedWinnerFirst baselineWinnerSecond transformedWinnerSecond : ℝ → Unit → Prop),
      (∀ (y : ℝ),
          PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ExactWinnerKKT (baselineWinnerFirst y)
            (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.baselineFreeTwoEdgeProbeKKT firstLoad y)) →
        (∀ (y : ℝ),
            PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ExactWinnerKKT (transformedWinnerFirst y)
              (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.transformedFreeTwoEdgeProbeKKT F firstLoad y)) →
          (∀ (y : ℝ), baselineWinnerFirst y = transformedWinnerFirst y) →
            (∀ (y : ℝ),
                PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ExactWinnerKKT (baselineWinnerSecond y)
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.baselineFreeTwoEdgeProbeKKT secondLoad y)) →
              (∀ (y : ℝ),
                  PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ExactWinnerKKT (transformedWinnerSecond y)
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.transformedFreeTwoEdgeProbeKKT F secondLoad y)) →
                (∀ (y : ℝ), baselineWinnerSecond y = transformedWinnerSecond y) →
                  Continuous F → F 0 = 0 → Irrational (firstLoad / secondLoad) → F = id) ∧
    ∀ (F : ℝ → ℝ) (lambda : ℝ) (baselineWinner transformedWinner : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → Prop),
      PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ExactStarWinnerKKT baselineWinner
          (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.baselineStarResidual lambda) →
        PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ExactStarWinnerKKT transformedWinner
            (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.transformedStarResidual F lambda) →
          baselineWinner = transformedWinner → Continuous F → Function.Odd F → lambda ≠ 0 → F = id

PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.flux_d2_rigidity_03 :
  (∀ (F : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ),
      Continuous F →
        F 0 = 0 →
          PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ShiftEquivariant F a →
            PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ShiftEquivariant F b → Irrational (a / b) → F = id) ∧
    ∀ (F : ℝ → ℝ) (lambda : ℝ),
      Continuous F → Function.Odd F → lambda ≠ 0 → PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.CompleteStarEquation F lambda → F = id

PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.flux_d3_general_04 {J K : ℕ} (hJK : J < K)
  (basis : PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.AnalyticPeriodicAuditBasis K) (score : Fin J → (ℝ → ℝ) →ₗ[ℝ] ℝ) :
  ∃ coefficient,
    coefficient ≠ 0 ∧
      (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteAuditMatrix basis score).mulVec coefficient = 0 ∧
        ∀ (scale : ℝ),
          scale ≠ 0 →
            |scale| * PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteModeDerivativeBudget basis coefficient < 1 →
              (∀ (x : ℝ), AnalyticAt ℝ (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteLedgerGauge basis coefficient scale) x) ∧
                StrictMono (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteLedgerGauge basis coefficient scale) ∧
                  PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ShiftEquivariant
                      (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteLedgerGauge basis coefficient scale) basis.period ∧
                    PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteLedgerGauge basis coefficient scale ≠ id ∧
                      (∀ (j : Fin J),
                          (score j) (scale • PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteModePerturbation basis coefficient) = 0) ∧
                        ∃ x, PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteLedgerGauge basis coefficient scale x ≠ x
PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.flux_d3_general_04_arbitrarily_close {J K : ℕ} (hJK : J < K)
  (basis : PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.AnalyticPeriodicAuditBasis K) (score : Fin J → (ℝ → ℝ) →ₗ[ℝ] ℝ) :
  ∃ coefficient,
    coefficient ≠ 0 ∧
      (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteAuditMatrix basis score).mulVec coefficient = 0 ∧
        ∀ (M : ℕ) (audit : Fin M → Seminorm ℝ (ℝ → ℝ)) (epsilon : ℝ),
          0 < epsilon →
            ∃ scale,
              scale ≠ 0 ∧
                |scale| * PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteModeDerivativeBudget basis coefficient < 1 ∧
                  (∀ (r : Fin M),
                      (audit r) (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteLedgerGauge basis coefficient scale - id) < epsilon) ∧
                    (∀ (x : ℝ), AnalyticAt ℝ (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteLedgerGauge basis coefficient scale) x) ∧
                      StrictMono (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteLedgerGauge basis coefficient scale) ∧
                        PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ShiftEquivariant
                            (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteLedgerGauge basis coefficient scale) basis.period ∧
                          PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteLedgerGauge basis coefficient scale ≠ id ∧
                            (∀ (j : Fin J),
                                (score j) (scale • PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteModePerturbation basis coefficient) =
                                  0) ∧
                              ∃ x, PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.finiteLedgerGauge basis coefficient scale x ≠ x

PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.flux_d4_complete_with_canonical_lambert {lambda p C : ℝ} {t : ℝ → ℝ} (hlambda : lambda ≠ 0)
  (hp : 1 < p) (hC : 0 < C) (r : ℕ) (htPos : ∀ᶠ (epsilon : ℝ) in nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0), 0 < t epsilon)
  (htZero : Filter.Tendsto t (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0)) (nhds 0))
  (hRemainder :
    Filter.Tendsto
      (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.contactNormalizedRemainder (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactResponseExponent r p) C
        t)
      (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0)) (nhds 0)) :
  (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactGauge lambda r 0 = 0 ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.ShiftEquivariant (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactGauge lambda r) lambda ∧
        StrictMono (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactGauge lambda r) ∧
          deriv (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactGauge lambda r) =
              PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.raisedCosineMarginal lambda r ∧
            analyticOrderAt
                (fun z =>
                  PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactGauge lambda r z -
                    PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactGauge lambda r 0)
                0 =
              ↑(2 * r + 1)) ∧
    Filter.Tendsto
        (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.contactRecoveredCoefficient
          (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactResponseExponent r p) t)
        (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0)) (nhds C) ∧
      Filter.Tendsto (fun epsilon => Real.log (t epsilon) / Real.log epsilon) (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0))
          (nhds (1 / PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactResponseExponent r p)) ∧
        Filter.Tendsto
            (fun epsilon =>
              t epsilon /
                PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.lambertNormalT epsilon
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactResponseExponent r p) C
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.contactLambertBranch
                    (PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactResponseExponent r p) C epsilon))
            (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0)) (nhds 1) ∧
          Asymptotics.IsEquivalent (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0))
            (fun epsilon =>
              t epsilon ^ PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.oddContactResponseExponent r p / -Real.log (t epsilon))
            fun epsilon => epsilon / C

PhonologicalCalculus.Flux.terminalKKT_accumulatingResponse_identifies.{u_1} {Theta : Type u_1} {domain : Set ℝ}
  {leftLaw rightLaw : ℝ → ℝ} {leftFlux rightFlux profile : Theta → ℕ → ℝ} {sample : Theta → ℝ} {mu epsilon : ℝ}
  {first last : ℕ} {base : ℝ} (hfirst : first ≤ last)
  (hleftStep :
    ∀ (parameter : Theta) (index : ℕ),
      leftFlux parameter index - leftFlux parameter (index + 1) =
        mu * (1 + epsilon) * profile parameter index ^ epsilon)
  (hrightStep :
    ∀ (parameter : Theta) (index : ℕ),
      rightFlux parameter index - rightFlux parameter (index + 1) =
        mu * (1 + epsilon) * profile parameter index ^ epsilon)
  (hleftTerminal : ∀ (parameter : Theta), leftFlux parameter (last + 1) = 0)
  (hrightTerminal : ∀ (parameter : Theta), rightFlux parameter (last + 1) = 0)
  (hleftSample : ∀ (parameter : Theta), leftFlux parameter first = leftLaw (sample parameter))
  (hrightSample : ∀ (parameter : Theta), rightFlux parameter first = rightLaw (sample parameter))
  (hleftAnalytic : AnalyticOnNhd ℝ leftLaw domain) (hrightAnalytic : AnalyticOnNhd ℝ rightLaw domain)
  (hdomain : IsPreconnected domain) (hbase : base ∈ domain)
  (haccumulation : ∃ᶠ (point : ℝ) in nhdsWithin base {base}ᶜ, ∃ parameter, sample parameter = point) :
  Set.EqOn leftLaw rightLaw domain

  (coNP : PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.EncodedDecisionClass) : Prop

PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.ExecutableQuarticCode.unitCommon_mul_sourceValue_le_tagLift
  (source : PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.ExecutableQuarticCode) (oldActivity : Fin source.coordinateCount → ℝ)
  (hOld : ∀ (coordinate : Fin source.coordinateCount), 0 ≤ oldActivity coordinate)
  (tagActivity : Fin source.unitTagDimension → ℝ)
  (hTag : ∀ (coordinate : Fin source.unitTagDimension), 0 < tagActivity coordinate ∧ tagActivity coordinate ≤ 1) :
  source.unitCommonTagMonomial tagActivity * source.unitSourceValue oldActivity ≤
    source.unitTagLiftValue oldActivity tagActivity

  (source : PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.ExecutableQuarticCode) (slot : source.UnitSlot) :
  (∀ (coordinate : Fin source.coordinateCount),
      1 ≤ ↑(source.unitTaggedResidualRow slot (finSumFinEquiv (Sum.inl coordinate))) + 1 ∧
        ↑(source.unitTaggedResidualRow slot (finSumFinEquiv (Sum.inl coordinate))) + 1 ≤ 5) ∧
    ∀ (coordinate : Fin source.unitTagDimension),
      1 ≤ ↑(source.unitTaggedResidualRow slot (finSumFinEquiv (Sum.inr coordinate))) + 1 ∧
        ↑(source.unitTaggedResidualRow slot (finSumFinEquiv (Sum.inr coordinate))) + 1 ≤ 3

  source.unitPositiveRows.card + source.unitNegativeRows.card = source.coefficientL1

PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.allWeightCrossInputProbabilityEqual_iff_relativeMeasure_eq.{uConstraint, uCandidate}
  {J : Type uConstraint} {C₁ C₂ : Type uCandidate} [DecidableEq J] [Fintype J] [Fintype C₁] [Fintype C₂] [Nonempty C₁]
  [Nonempty C₂] (first : PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.CompleteFiniteLedger J C₁) (namedFirst : C₁)
  (second : PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.CompleteFiniteLedger J C₂) (namedSecond : C₂) :

      PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.IsNonnegativeWeight weight →

          PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.exponentialCandidateProbability second weight namedSecond) ↔
    PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.relativeRowMassMeasure first namedFirst =
      PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.relativeRowMassMeasure second namedSecond

  {J : Type uConstraint} {C : Type uCandidate} [Fintype J] [DecidableEq J] [Fintype C] [Nonempty C]
  (ledger : PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.CompleteFiniteLedger J C) (first second : C)

PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.allWeightSameInputProbabilityOrder_iff_row_domination.{uConstraint, uCandidate}
  {J : Type uConstraint} {C : Type uCandidate} [Fintype J] [DecidableEq J] [Fintype C] [Nonempty C]

      PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.IsNonnegativeWeight weight →
