    PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.RowDominates (ledger.row first) (ledger.row second)
PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.balancedInterior_probability_translation (c r : ℕ) {activity : ℝ}
  (hactivity : 0 ≤ activity) :
  have positiveMass :=
    PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.positiveCoefficientMass (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.balancedInteriorPolynomial c r)
      activity;
  have negativeMass :=
    PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.negativeCoefficientMass (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.balancedInteriorPolynomial c r)
      activity;
  PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.namedCandidateProbability negativeMass -
        PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.namedCandidateProbability positiveMass =
      Polynomial.eval activity (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.balancedInteriorPolynomial c r) /
        ((1 + positiveMass) * (1 + negativeMass)) ∧
    0 < (1 + positiveMass) * (1 + negativeMass) ∧
      (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.namedCandidateProbability negativeMass -
              PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.namedCandidateProbability positiveMass =
            0 ↔
          Polynomial.eval activity (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.balancedInteriorPolynomial c r) = 0) ∧
        (0 <
            PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.namedCandidateProbability negativeMass -
              PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.namedCandidateProbability positiveMass ↔
          0 < Polynomial.eval activity (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.balancedInteriorPolynomial c r))

                  PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.compiledUnitBaseMass candidate = 1 ∧
                    0 < PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.compiledUnitBaseMass candidate

PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.contractionStrictifier_exists_negative_iff_source_zero.{u_1} {U : Type u_1}
  (domain : U → Prop) (residualObjective : U → ℝ) (m : ℕ) (gap : ℝ)
  (_hresidualNonneg : ∀ (u : U), domain u → 0 ≤ residualObjective u)
  (hgapIfNoZero : (¬∃ u, domain u ∧ residualObjective u = 0) → ∀ (u : U), domain u → gap ≤ residualObjective u)
  (hterminal : 2 * PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.exactContractionChain m ^ 2 < gap) :
  (∃ u, domain u ∧ residualObjective u = 0) ↔
    ∃ u chain,
      domain u ∧
        chain 0 = 1 ∧
          (∀ i ≤ m, 0 ≤ chain i ∧ chain i ≤ 1) ∧
            PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.contractionStrictifier residualObjective m u chain < 0
PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.contraction_terminal_square_bound (chain : ℕ → ℝ) (m : ℕ) (hzero : chain 0 = 1)
  (hcube : ∀ i ≤ m, 0 ≤ chain i ∧ chain i ≤ 1) :
  chain m ^ 2 ≤
    2 * PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.exactContractionChain m ^ 2 +
      ↑m / 2 * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.contractionResidual chain i ^ 2

PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.evaluateRationalNamedCrossCarrier_eq_margin.{uConstraint, uCandidate} {J : Type uConstraint}
  {C₁ C₂ : Type uCandidate} [DecidableEq J] [Fintype J] [Fintype C₁] [Fintype C₂] (baseMass₁ : C₁ → ℚ)
  (row₁ : C₁ → PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.LaurentExponent J) (named₁ : C₁) (baseMass₂ : C₂ → ℚ)
  (row₂ : C₂ → PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.LaurentExponent J) (named₂ : C₂) (activity : J → ℝ)
  (hActivity : ∀ (coordinate : J), activity coordinate ≠ 0) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.evaluateRationalSparseLaurent
      (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.rationalNamedCrossCarrier baseMass₁ row₁ named₁ baseMass₂ row₂ named₂) activity =
    PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.candidateMass (fun candidate => ↑(baseMass₂ candidate)) row₂ activity named₂ *
        PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.partitionMass (fun candidate => ↑(baseMass₁ candidate)) row₁ activity -
      PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.candidateMass (fun candidate => ↑(baseMass₁ candidate)) row₁ activity named₁ *
        PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.partitionMass (fun candidate => ↑(baseMass₂ candidate)) row₂ activity

PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.exists_canonicalCollectedResponseEnumeration.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {C : Type u_1} {R : Type u_2}
  {X : Type u_3} [Fintype C] [DecidableEq R] (row : C → R) (coefficient : C → ℝ) (kernel : R → X → ℝ) :

    Function.Injective node ∧
      (∀ (i : Fin n), collectedCoefficient i ≠ 0) ∧
        ∀ (argument : X),
          ∑ candidate, coefficient candidate * kernel (row candidate) argument =
            ∑ i, collectedCoefficient i * kernel (node i) argument

PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.max_g1_sparseCarrier_minimalClearing.{uConstraint, uCandidate} {J : Type uConstraint}
  {C₁ C₂ : Type uCandidate} [DecidableEq J] [Fintype J] [Fintype C₁] [Fintype C₂]
  (row₁ : C₁ → PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.LaurentExponent J)
  (row₂ : C₂ → PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.LaurentExponent J) :
  (∀ (exponent : PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.LaurentExponent J),
      (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂) exponent =
        PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.exponentMultiplicity row₁ exponent -
          PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.exponentMultiplicity row₂ exponent) ∧
    (∀ exponent ∈ (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂).support,
        ∀ (coordinate : J),
          0 ≤
            exponent coordinate +
              ↑(PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.minimalClearingShift
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂) coordinate)) ∧
      (∀ (shift : J → ℕ),
          (∀ exponent ∈ (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂).support,
              ∀ (coordinate : J), 0 ≤ exponent coordinate + ↑(shift coordinate)) →
            ∀ (coordinate : J),
              PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.minimalClearingShift
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂) coordinate ≤
                shift coordinate) ∧
        PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseClearedPolynomial
            (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂) =
          ∑ exponent ∈ (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂).support,
            (MvPolynomial.monomial
                (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.clearedExponent
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂) exponent))
              ((PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂) exponent)
PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.max_g1_sparsePolynomialCarrier.{uConstraint, uCandidate} {J : Type uConstraint}
  {C₁ C₂ : Type uCandidate} [DecidableEq J] [Fintype J] [Fintype C₁] [Fintype C₂]
  (row₁ : C₁ → PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.LaurentExponent J)
  (row₂ : C₂ → PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.LaurentExponent J) (activity : J → ℝ)
  (hActivity : ∀ (coordinate : J), 0 < activity coordinate) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.evaluateSparseLaurent (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂)
        activity =
      ∑ candidate, ∏ coordinate, activity coordinate ^ row₁ candidate coordinate -
        ∑ candidate, ∏ coordinate, activity coordinate ^ row₂ candidate coordinate ∧
    (0 ≤
          MvPolynomial.eval₂ (Int.castRingHom ℝ) activity
            (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseClearedPolynomial
              (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂)) ↔
        0 ≤
          PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.evaluateSparseLaurent
            (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂) activity) ∧
      (MvPolynomial.eval₂ (Int.castRingHom ℝ) activity
              (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseClearedPolynomial
                (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂)) =
            0 ↔
          PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.evaluateSparseLaurent
              (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂) activity =
            0) ∧
        (0 <
            MvPolynomial.eval₂ (Int.castRingHom ℝ) activity
              (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseClearedPolynomial
                (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂)) ↔
          0 <
            PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.evaluateSparseLaurent
              (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.sparseRelativeCarrier row₁ row₂) activity)

          0 < z →
            z ≤ 1 →
              PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.normalizedBinaryLaw (1 + z + z ^ 2) (z ^ 3) ≠
                  PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.normalizedBinaryLaw (1 + z ^ 2) (z ^ 3) ∧
                (1 + z + z ^ 2) * (1 + z ^ 2 + z ^ 3) - (1 + z ^ 2) * (1 + z + z ^ 2 + z ^ 3) = z ^ 4 ∧
                  0 < (1 + z) * (1 + z ^ 2) * (1 + z ^ 2 + z ^ 3)
PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.max_g9_lawtoenv_01.{u_1} {K : Type u_1} [Field K] [LinearOrder K] [IsStrictOrderedRing K] :
  (∀ (z : K),
      0 < z →
        z ≤ 1 →
          PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.normalizedBinaryLaw 1 z =
            PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.normalizedBinaryLaw (1 + z ^ 2) (z + z ^ 3)) ∧
    PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.responseEnvelopeOfRows [0] [1] = some (1, 1) ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.responseEnvelopeOfRows [0, 2] [1, 3] = some (-1, 3) ∧
        PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.responseEnvelopeOfRows [0] [1] ≠
          PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.responseEnvelopeOfRows [0, 2] [1, 3]
PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.max_g9_positiveMassResponseSet_eq_iff_envelope_eq.{u_1} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] (leftOne rightOne leftTwo rightTwo : Finset E)
  (hleftOne : leftOne.Nonempty) (hrightOne : rightOne.Nonempty) (hleftTwo : leftTwo.Nonempty)
  (hrightTwo : rightTwo.Nonempty) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.positiveMassResponseSet leftOne rightOne =
      PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.positiveMassResponseSet leftTwo rightTwo ↔
    PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.convexHullResponseEnvelope leftOne rightOne =
      PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.convexHullResponseEnvelope leftTwo rightTwo

  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] (left right : Finset E) (hleft : left.Nonempty)
  (hright : right.Nonempty) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.positiveMassRobustSignDirections left right =
    PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.nonnegativePolar (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.convexHullResponseEnvelope left right)

PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.mixedResponseJetZeroThrough_mul_iff_right.{u_1} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] (denominator response : E → ℝ) (base : E) (order : ℕ) (hdenominator : ContDiff ℝ ⊤ denominator)
  (hresponse : ContDiff ℝ ⊤ response) (hdenominatorNe : ∀ (weight : E), denominator weight ≠ 0) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.mixedResponseJetZeroThrough (fun weight => denominator weight * response weight) base
      order ↔
    PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.mixedResponseJetZeroThrough response base order

    (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.CollectedExponentialPresentation

        consequence₂ answer))

PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.rationalClearedIntegerPolynomial_sign_iff.{uConstraint} {J : Type uConstraint}
  [DecidableEq J] [Fintype J] (carrier : PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.LaurentExponent J →₀ ℚ) (activity : J → ℝ)
  (hActivity : ∀ (coordinate : J), 0 < activity coordinate) :
  (0 ≤
        MvPolynomial.eval₂ (Int.castRingHom ℝ) activity
          (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.rationalClearedIntegerPolynomial carrier) ↔
      0 ≤ PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.evaluateRationalSparseLaurent carrier activity) ∧
    (MvPolynomial.eval₂ (Int.castRingHom ℝ) activity
            (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.rationalClearedIntegerPolynomial carrier) =
          0 ↔
        PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.evaluateRationalSparseLaurent carrier activity = 0) ∧
      (0 <
          MvPolynomial.eval₂ (Int.castRingHom ℝ) activity
            (PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.rationalClearedIntegerPolynomial carrier) ↔
        0 < PhonologicalCalculus.MaxEnt.evaluateRationalSparseLaurent carrier activity)

PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseImport.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} (Source : Type u_1) (Code : Type u_2) (Semantic : Type u_3)
  (Answer : Type u_4) [DecidableEq Code] : Type (max (max (max u_1 u_2) u_3) u_4)
PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseImport.mem_mismatchCodes_iff.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {Source : Type u_1} {Code : Type u_2}
  {Semantic : Type u_3} {Answer : Type u_4} [DecidableEq Code] [DecidableEq Answer]
  (bridge : PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseImport Source Code Semantic Answer) (code : Code) :
  code ∈ bridge.mismatchCodes ↔ code ∈ bridge.reachable ∧ bridge.reader code ≠ bridge.reader (bridge.descent code)
PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseImport.mismatch_has_source.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {Source : Type u_1} {Code : Type u_2}
  {Semantic : Type u_3} {Answer : Type u_4} [DecidableEq Code] [DecidableEq Answer]
  (bridge : PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseImport Source Code Semantic Answer) {code : Code}
  (hmismatch : code ∈ bridge.mismatchCodes) : ∃ source, bridge.encode source = code
PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseImport.query_square.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {Source : Type u_1} {Code : Type u_2}
  {Semantic : Type u_3} {Answer : Type u_4} [DecidableEq Code]
  (bridge : PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseImport Source Code Semantic Answer) (source : Source) :
  bridge.reader (bridge.descent (bridge.encode source)) =
    bridge.semanticQuery (bridge.semanticTransform (bridge.response source))
PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseImport.reader_encode.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {Source : Type u_1} {Code : Type u_2}
  {Semantic : Type u_3} {Answer : Type u_4} [DecidableEq Code]
  (bridge : PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseImport Source Code Semantic Answer) (source : Source) :
  bridge.reader (bridge.encode source) = bridge.semanticQuery (bridge.response source)
PhonologicalCalculus.Selection.exists_open_spherical_gap_of_not_positiveRayFamily.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι]
  {target : ι → ℝ} {normals : List (ι → ℝ)} (htargetNonzero : target ≠ 0)
  (hnotrays : ¬PhonologicalCalculus.Selection.PositiveRayFamily target normals) :
  ∃ gap,
    IsOpen gap ∧
      gap.Nonempty ∧
        gap ⊆
          PhonologicalCalculus.Selection.sphericalPairwiseEvent target \
            PhonologicalCalculus.Selection.sphericalSelectedEvent normals
PhonologicalCalculus.Selection.exists_positive_twoShell_reversal {a b c : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (hc : 0 < c) :
  ∃ epsilon,
    0 < epsilon ∧
      epsilon < 1 ∧
        0 < (1 - epsilon) * a + epsilon * b ∧ 0 < epsilon * c ∧ epsilon * c < (1 - epsilon) * a + epsilon * b

PhonologicalCalculus.Support.endpointWinner_supportClassification.{u_1} {Candidate : Type u_1} {edge site : ℝ → ℝ}
  {horizon positiveLength : Candidate → ℕ} {flux : Candidate → ℕ → ℝ}
  {boundaryPrevious boundaryIncomingSlope : Candidate → ℝ} {siteSlope edgeSlopeAtOne : ℝ} (winner : Candidate → Prop)
  (hProof :
    PhonologicalCalculus.Support.ExactEndpointWinnerKKT winner fun candidate =>
      PhonologicalCalculus.Support.EndpointPathKKT edge site (horizon candidate) (positiveLength candidate)
        (flux candidate) siteSlope edgeSlopeAtOne (boundaryPrevious candidate) (boundaryIncomingSlope candidate)) :
  (siteSlope = 0 → ∀ (candidate : Candidate), winner candidate → positiveLength candidate = horizon candidate) ∧
    (0 < siteSlope →
      ∀ (candidate : Candidate),
        winner candidate →
          ↑(positiveLength candidate) * siteSlope < edgeSlopeAtOne ∧
            positiveLength candidate ≤ ⌈edgeSlopeAtOne / siteSlope⌉₊ - 1)
PhonologicalCalculus.Support.existsUnique_matchedPowerDecayRatio (h m p : ℝ) (hPositive : 0 < h) (mPositive : 0 < m)
  (pGreaterOne : 1 < p) :
  ∃! lambda, 0 < lambda ∧ lambda < 1 ∧ PhonologicalCalculus.Support.MatchedPowerDecayEquation h m p lambda
PhonologicalCalculus.Support.flux_drop_strict_prefix_bound {flux : ℕ → ℝ} {siteSlope edgeSlopeAtOne : ℝ} {K : ℕ}
  (hdrop : ∀ i < K, flux (i + 1) + siteSlope ≤ flux i) (hterminal : 0 < flux K) (hinitial : flux 0 ≤ edgeSlopeAtOne) :
  ↑K * siteSlope < edgeSlopeAtOne

PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPower_bellman_equality_iff (h m p lambda a b : ℝ) (hPositive : 0 < h)
  (_mPositive : 0 < m) (pGreaterOne : 1 < p) (lambdaPositive : 0 < lambda) (lambdaBelowOne : lambda < 1)
  (aNonnegative : 0 ≤ a) (bNonnegative : 0 ≤ b)
  (decay : PhonologicalCalculus.Support.MatchedPowerDecayEquation h m p lambda) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPowerEdgeCost h m p a b +
        PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPowerContinuationCoefficient h p lambda * b ^ p =
      PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPowerContinuationCoefficient h p lambda * a ^ p ↔
    b = lambda * a
PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPower_bellman_lower_bound (h m p lambda a b : ℝ) (hPositive : 0 < h)
  (_mPositive : 0 < m) (pGreaterOne : 1 < p) (lambdaPositive : 0 < lambda) (lambdaBelowOne : lambda < 1)
  (aNonnegative : 0 ≤ a) (bNonnegative : 0 ≤ b)
  (decay : PhonologicalCalculus.Support.MatchedPowerDecayEquation h m p lambda) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPowerContinuationCoefficient h p lambda * a ^ p ≤
    PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPowerEdgeCost h m p a b +
      PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPowerContinuationCoefficient h p lambda * b ^ p
PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPower_continuationCoefficient_unique (h m p lambda : ℝ)
  (lambdaPositive : 0 < lambda) (decay : PhonologicalCalculus.Support.MatchedPowerDecayEquation h m p lambda) :
  ∃! coefficient,
    coefficient = PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPowerContinuationCoefficient h p lambda ∧
      coefficient = PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPowerContinuationCoefficient h p lambda / lambda ^ (p - 1) - m
PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPower_finite_continuation_lower_bound (h m p lambda : ℝ) (profile : ℕ → ℝ)
  (horizon : ℕ) (hPositive : 0 < h) (mPositive : 0 < m) (pGreaterOne : 1 < p) (lambdaPositive : 0 < lambda)
  (lambdaBelowOne : lambda < 1) (decay : PhonologicalCalculus.Support.MatchedPowerDecayEquation h m p lambda)

    PhonologicalCalculus.Support.matchedPowerFiniteContinuationCost h m p lambda profile horizon

  have root := (3 - √5) / 2;
  0 < root ∧
    root ≤ 1 / 2 ∧ root ^ 2 - 3 * root + 1 = 0 ∧ ∀ (x : ℝ), 0 ≤ x → x ≤ 1 / 2 → x ^ 2 - 3 * x + 1 = 0 → x = root

  PhonologicalCalculus.AddedConsumerPrice first added = 0 ↔ PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines first added
PhonologicalCalculus.added_consumer_can_strictly_refine :
  PhonologicalCalculus.SameUnder PhonologicalCalculus.constantQuery PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.left
      PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.right ∧
    ¬PhonologicalCalculus.SameUnder
        (fun x => (PhonologicalCalculus.constantQuery x, PhonologicalCalculus.identityQuery x))
        PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.left PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.right
PhonologicalCalculus.adding_consumer_refines_kernel.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {X : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2} {B : Type u_3}
  (first : X → A) (added : X → B) : PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines (fun x => (first x, added x)) first

PhonologicalCalculus.closedBattery_intersection.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Consumer : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {Answer : Consumer → Type u_3} [DecidableEq Consumer] (consumerUniverse : Finset Consumer)
  (answers : (consumer : Consumer) → X → Answer consumer) {first second : Finset Consumer}
  (hFirst : PhonologicalCalculus.ClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers first)
  (hSecond : PhonologicalCalculus.ClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers second) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers (first ∩ second)
PhonologicalCalculus.closedBattery_join.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Consumer : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {Answer : Consumer → Type u_3} [DecidableEq Consumer] (consumerUniverse : Finset Consumer)
  (answers : (consumer : Consumer) → X → Answer consumer) {first second : Finset Consumer}
  (hFirst : PhonologicalCalculus.ClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers first)
  (hSecond : PhonologicalCalculus.ClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers second) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers
    (PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers (first ∪ second))
PhonologicalCalculus.collisionSet_comp_decomposition.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {X : Type u_1} {Y : Type u_2} {Z : Type u_3}
  [Fintype X] [DecidableEq X] [DecidableEq Y] [DecidableEq Z] (first : X → Y) (second : Y → Z) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.CollisionSet (second ∘ first) =
    PhonologicalCalculus.CollisionSet first ∪ PhonologicalCalculus.NewCollisionSet first second
PhonologicalCalculus.collisionSet_comp_decomposition_disjoint.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {X : Type u_1} {Y : Type u_2}
  {Z : Type u_3} [Fintype X] [DecidableEq X] [DecidableEq Y] [DecidableEq Z] (first : X → Y) (second : Y → Z) :
  Disjoint (PhonologicalCalculus.CollisionSet first) (PhonologicalCalculus.NewCollisionSet first second)
PhonologicalCalculus.completeManySortedContextRelation_isGreatest.{uSort, uCarrier, uObservation, u_1}
  {SortIndex : Type uSort} {Carrier : SortIndex → Type uCarrier}
  (signature : PhonologicalCalculus.FinitarySortedSignature SortIndex)
  (operations : PhonologicalCalculus.FinitarySortedOperations Carrier signature)

  PhonologicalCalculus.IsGreatestSortedContextualRelation signature operations
    (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedObservationKernel Observation observe)
    (PhonologicalCalculus.CompleteManySortedContextRelation signature operations Observation observe)

  {Carrier : SortIndex → Type uCarrier} (signature : PhonologicalCalculus.FinitarySortedSignature SortIndex)
  (operations : PhonologicalCalculus.FinitarySortedOperations Carrier signature)

  (hRelated :
    ∀ (position : Fin (signature.arity operation)),
      relation (signature.inputSort operation position) (first position) (second position))
  (hDifferent : (operations operation first).isSome ≠ (operations operation second).isSome) :
  ¬PhonologicalCalculus.ManySortedStrongCongruence signature operations relation

  ¬PhonologicalCalculus.StrongPartialCongruence operations relation

PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a1_inverse_01 :
  PhonologicalCalculus.CollisionSet PhonologicalCalculus.orbitIdentity = ∅ ∧
    Function.Injective PhonologicalCalculus.orbitIdentity ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.HasInverseOnRange PhonologicalCalculus.orbitIdentity ∧
        PhonologicalCalculus.HasTwoSidedInverseOnRange PhonologicalCalculus.orbitIdentity

  PhonologicalCalculus.CollisionSet PhonologicalCalculus.orbitCollision ≠ ∅ ∧
    ¬Function.Injective PhonologicalCalculus.orbitCollision ∧
      ¬PhonologicalCalculus.HasInverseOnRange PhonologicalCalculus.orbitCollision ∧
        ¬PhonologicalCalculus.HasTwoSidedInverseOnRange PhonologicalCalculus.orbitCollision
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a1_semanticOrbitRecovery_from_weakening.{u_1, u_2} {X : Type u_1} {Y : Type u_2}
  (source : Setoid X) (target : Setoid Y) (weakening : X → Y)
  (respects : PhonologicalCalculus.OrbitMapRespects source target weakening) [Fintype (Quotient source)]
  [DecidableEq (Quotient source)] [DecidableEq (Quotient target)] [Nonempty X] :
  have orbitMap := PhonologicalCalculus.inducedOrbitMap source target weakening respects;
  (PhonologicalCalculus.CollisionSet orbitMap = ∅ ↔ PhonologicalCalculus.HasTwoSidedInverseOnRange orbitMap) ∧
    (Set.range orbitMap).Nonempty
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a2_coherence_03 :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ActionLiftCoherent id PhonologicalCalculus.identityBoolAction
      PhonologicalCalculus.identityBoolAction ∧
    (∀ (value : Bool), PhonologicalCalculus.identityBoolAction false value = value) ∧
      ∀ (first second value : Bool),
        PhonologicalCalculus.identityBoolAction first (PhonologicalCalculus.identityBoolAction second value) =
          PhonologicalCalculus.identityBoolAction second value

    ¬PhonologicalCalculus.TargetImageStable PhonologicalCalculus.outboundWeakening
        PhonologicalCalculus.outboundTargetSwap
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a2_proof_01 :
  PhonologicalCalculus.RepresentativeRecoveryExists id PhonologicalCalculus.identityBoolAction
    PhonologicalCalculus.identityBoolAction
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a2_representativeRecovery.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {A : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {Y : Type u_3}
  (weakening : X → Y) (sourceAction : A → X → X) (targetAction : A → Y → Y) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.RepresentativeRecoveryExists weakening sourceAction targetAction ↔
    Function.Injective weakening ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.TargetImageStable weakening targetAction ∧
        PhonologicalCalculus.ActionLiftCoherent weakening sourceAction targetAction

  PhonologicalCalculus.registeredSerialEdgeCases =
    { emptyWordMass := 1 / 4, summedWordMass := 1, deadlockState := "dead", structuralCycle := true,
      probabilityInfinityMass := 0 }
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a3_marginals_01 :
  PhonologicalCalculus.firstBinaryMarginal PhonologicalCalculus.jointLawDiagonal =
      PhonologicalCalculus.firstBinaryMarginal PhonologicalCalculus.jointLawOffDiagonal ∧
    PhonologicalCalculus.secondBinaryMarginal PhonologicalCalculus.jointLawDiagonal =
        PhonologicalCalculus.secondBinaryMarginal PhonologicalCalculus.jointLawOffDiagonal ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.jointLawDiagonal ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.jointLawOffDiagonal
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a3_nontermination_02 :

      PhonologicalCalculus.halfStoppingLaw ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.certainStoppingLaw
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a3_typedSerialDecisions
  (castRegistry : PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag → PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag → Bool) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.allSerialTags.Nodup ∧
    (∀ (source target : PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag),
        source ≠ target →
          castRegistry source target = false →
            ¬PhonologicalCalculus.SerialComparisonAdmitted castRegistry source target) ∧
      (∀ (first second : PhonologicalCalculus.UnaryStoppedLaw),
          first.infinityMass ≠ second.infinityMass → first ≠ second) ∧
        (∀ (coefficients : List ℚ),
            PhonologicalCalculus.AdmitsProbabilitySemantics coefficients ↔
              PhonologicalCalculus.WeightedSeriesNormalized coefficients) ∧
          (castRegistry PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.allPrefixes PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.stoppedSet = false →
            ¬PhonologicalCalculus.SerialComparisonAdmitted castRegistry PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.allPrefixes
                PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.stoppedSet)
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a3_types_04 :
  PhonologicalCalculus.allSerialTags.length = 4 ∧ PhonologicalCalculus.allSerialTags.Nodup
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a4_converse_boundary :
  (PhonologicalCalculus.constantQuery PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.left =
        PhonologicalCalculus.constantQuery PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.right ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.identityQuery PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.left ≠
        PhonologicalCalculus.identityQuery PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.right) ∧
    PhonologicalCalculus.SameUnder PhonologicalCalculus.constantQuery PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.left
        PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.right ∧
      ¬PhonologicalCalculus.SameUnder
          (fun x => (PhonologicalCalculus.constantQuery x, PhonologicalCalculus.identityQuery x))
          PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.left PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.right
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a4_query_consumer_monotonicity.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4, u_5, u_6} {X : Type u_1}
  {Strong : Type u_2} {Weak : Type u_3} {Reduction : Type u_4} {A : Type u_5} {B : Type u_6} [Fintype X]
  (strong : X → Strong) (weak : X → Weak) (reduction : X → Reduction) (first : X → A) (added : X → B) :
  (∀ (g : Strong → Weak),
      weak = g ∘ strong →
        PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines strong weak ∧
          (PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines reduction strong → PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines reduction weak)) ∧
    (∀ (x y : X),
        PhonologicalCalculus.SameUnder (fun z => (first z, added z)) x y ↔
          PhonologicalCalculus.SameUnder first x y ∧ PhonologicalCalculus.SameUnder added x y) ∧
      ((∃ g, ∀ (x : X), g ⟨strong x, ⋯⟩ = weak x) ↔ PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines strong weak)
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a5_carrierAlgebra.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4, u_5, u_6} {X : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2} {B : Type u_3}
  {S : Type u_4} {Q : Type u_5} {O : Type u_6} [Fintype X] (first : X → A) (added : X → B) (reduction : X → S)
  (query : X → Q) (suffix : S → O) :
  (Nat.card ↑(Set.range fun x => (first x, added x)) = Nat.card ↑(Set.range first) ↔
      PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines first added) ∧
    (PhonologicalCalculus.AddedConsumerPrice first added = 0 ↔ PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines first added) ∧
      ∀ (x y : X),
        reduction x = reduction y →
          query x ≠ query y → suffix (reduction x) = suffix (reduction y) ∧ ¬∃ reader, query = reader ∘ reduction
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a5_loss_03 :
  PhonologicalCalculus.FirstLossAt PhonologicalCalculus.registeredTwoStageReduction
    PhonologicalCalculus.registeredTwoStageQuery 1

      2 ∧
    PhonologicalCalculus.BlockwiseAddedConsumerPrice PhonologicalCalculus.priceFixtureFirst
        PhonologicalCalculus.priceFixtureAdded =
      2
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a6_arbitraryFiniteInformationLattice.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Consumer : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {Answer : Consumer → Type u_3} [DecidableEq Consumer] (consumerUniverse : Finset Consumer)
  (answers : (consumer : Consumer) → X → Answer consumer) :
  Nonempty (Fintype (PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers)) ∧
    ∀ (first second : PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers),
      ↑↑(first ⊓ second) = ↑↑first ∩ ↑↑second ∧
        ↑↑(first ⊔ second) = PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers (↑↑first ∪ ↑↑second)
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a6_constructedArbitraryFinitaryManySortedContextual.{uSort, uCarrier, uObservation, u_1}
  {SortIndex : Type uSort} {Carrier : SortIndex → Type uCarrier}
  (signature : PhonologicalCalculus.FinitarySortedSignature SortIndex)
  (operations : PhonologicalCalculus.FinitarySortedOperations Carrier signature)
  (FirstObservation SecondObservation : SortIndex → Type uObservation)
  (firstObserve : (sort : SortIndex) → Carrier sort → FirstObservation sort)
  (secondObserve : (sort : SortIndex) → Carrier sort → SecondObservation sort) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.IsGreatestSortedContextualRelation signature operations
      (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedObservationKernel FirstObservation firstObserve)
      (PhonologicalCalculus.CompleteManySortedContextRelation signature operations FirstObservation firstObserve) ∧
    PhonologicalCalculus.IsGreatestSortedContextualRelation signature operations
        (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedObservationKernel SecondObservation secondObserve)
        (PhonologicalCalculus.CompleteManySortedContextRelation signature operations SecondObservation secondObserve) ∧
      ∀ (sort : SortIndex) (first second : Carrier sort),
        PhonologicalCalculus.CompleteManySortedContextRelation signature operations
            (fun sort => FirstObservation sort × SecondObservation sort)
            (fun sort value => (firstObserve sort value, secondObserve sort value)) sort first second ↔
          PhonologicalCalculus.CompleteManySortedContextRelation signature operations FirstObservation firstObserve sort
              first second ∧
            PhonologicalCalculus.CompleteManySortedContextRelation signature operations SecondObservation secondObserve
              sort first second
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a6_contextualIntersectionAndDefinedness.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Operation : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {Index : Type u_3} [Nonempty Index] (operations : Operation → X → Option X) (relations : Index → X → X → Prop)
  (hStrong : ∀ (index : Index), PhonologicalCalculus.StrongPartialCongruence operations (relations index)) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.StrongPartialCongruence operations (PhonologicalCalculus.RelationIntersection relations) ∧
    ∀ (relation : X → X → Prop) (operation : Operation) (x y : X),
      relation x y →
        (operations operation x).isSome ≠ (operations operation y).isSome →
          ¬PhonologicalCalculus.StrongPartialCongruence operations relation
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a6_definedness_03 :
  PhonologicalCalculus.universalBoolRelation false true ∧
    ¬PhonologicalCalculus.StrongPartialCongruence PhonologicalCalculus.definednessFixtureOperation
        PhonologicalCalculus.universalBoolRelation
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a6_directInformationLattice.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Consumer : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {Answer : Consumer → Type u_3} [DecidableEq Consumer] (consumerUniverse : Finset Consumer)
  (answers : (consumer : Consumer) → X → Answer consumer) (battery : Finset Consumer)
  (hBattery : battery ⊆ consumerUniverse) :
  battery ⊆ PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers battery ∧
    (∀ (smaller larger : Finset Consumer),
        smaller ⊆ larger →
          PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers smaller ⊆
            PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers larger) ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers
            (PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers battery) =
          PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers battery ∧
        ∀ (first second : Finset Consumer),
          PhonologicalCalculus.ClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers first →
            PhonologicalCalculus.ClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers second →
              PhonologicalCalculus.ClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers (first ∩ second) ∧
                PhonologicalCalculus.ClosedBattery consumerUniverse answers
                  (PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers (first ∪ second))
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a6_finiteManySortedContextualRefinement_metaproof.{uSort, uCarrier, uObservation, u_1}
  {SortIndex : Type uSort} {Carrier : SortIndex → Type uCarrier} [Fintype SortIndex] [DecidableEq SortIndex]
  [(sort : SortIndex) → Fintype (Carrier sort)] [(sort : SortIndex) → DecidableEq (Carrier sort)]
  (signature : PhonologicalCalculus.FinitarySortedSignature SortIndex)
  (operations : PhonologicalCalculus.FinitarySortedOperations Carrier signature)
  (Observation : SortIndex → Type uObservation) (observe : (sort : SortIndex) → Carrier sort → Observation sort)
  (step :
    PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRelation (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedCarrierPoint Carrier) →
      PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRelation (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedCarrierPoint Carrier))
  (hContracting :
    ∀ (relation : PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRelation (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedCarrierPoint Carrier)),
      step relation ⊆ relation)
  (hTargetPreserved :
    ∀ (relation : PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRelation (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedCarrierPoint Carrier)),
      PhonologicalCalculus.finiteSortedRelationGraph
            (PhonologicalCalculus.CompleteManySortedContextRelation signature operations Observation observe) ⊆
          relation →
        PhonologicalCalculus.finiteSortedRelationGraph
            (PhonologicalCalculus.CompleteManySortedContextRelation signature operations Observation observe) ⊆
          step relation)
  (hTerminalSound :
    ∀ (relation : PhonologicalCalculus.FiniteRelation (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedCarrierPoint Carrier)),
      step relation = relation →
        relation ⊆
          PhonologicalCalculus.finiteSortedRelationGraph
            (PhonologicalCalculus.CompleteManySortedContextRelation signature operations Observation observe)) :
  ∃
    n ≤
      Finset.card
        (PhonologicalCalculus.finiteSortedRelationGraph
          (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedObservationKernel Observation observe)),
    PhonologicalCalculus.refinementIterate step n
          (PhonologicalCalculus.finiteSortedRelationGraph
            (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedObservationKernel Observation observe)) =
        PhonologicalCalculus.finiteSortedRelationGraph
          (PhonologicalCalculus.CompleteManySortedContextRelation signature operations Observation observe) ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.refinementIterate step (n + 1)
          (PhonologicalCalculus.finiteSortedRelationGraph
            (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedObservationKernel Observation observe)) =
        PhonologicalCalculus.refinementIterate step n
          (PhonologicalCalculus.finiteSortedRelationGraph
            (PhonologicalCalculus.SortedObservationKernel Observation observe))
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a6_lattice_01 :
  (¬PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines (fun value => value.1) fun value => value.2) ∧
    (¬PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines (fun value => value.2) fun value => value.1) ∧
      ∀ (x y : Bool × Bool), (x.1, x.2) = (y.1, y.2) ↔ x = y
PhonologicalCalculus.fin_a7_responseImport.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {Source : Type u_1} {Code : Type u_2}
  {Semantic : Type u_3} {Answer : Type u_4} [DecidableEq Code] [DecidableEq Answer]
  (bridge : PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseImport Source Code Semantic Answer) :
  (∀ (source : Source), bridge.reader (bridge.encode source) = bridge.semanticQuery (bridge.response source)) ∧
    (∀ (first second : Semantic), bridge.semanticEq first second = true ↔ first = second) ∧
      (∀ (source : Source),
          bridge.reader (bridge.descent (bridge.encode source)) =
            bridge.semanticQuery (bridge.semanticTransform (bridge.response source))) ∧
        ∀ code ∈ bridge.mismatchCodes, ∃ source, bridge.encode source = code

  [Fintype FiniteSource] [DecidableEq FiniteCode] [LinearOrder FiniteSource] (encode : FiniteSource → FiniteCode)
  (code : ↑(Set.range encode)) {source : FiniteSource} (hsource : encode source = ↑code) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.finiteLeastSection encode code ≤ source

  [Fintype FiniteSource] [DecidableEq FiniteCode] [LinearOrder FiniteSource] (encode : FiniteSource → FiniteCode)

PhonologicalCalculus.finiteOrbitRecovery_equivalences.{u_1, u_2} {X : Type u_1} {Y : Type u_2} [Fintype X]
  [DecidableEq X] [DecidableEq Y] (orbitMap : X → Y) :
  (PhonologicalCalculus.CollisionSet orbitMap = ∅ ↔ Function.Injective orbitMap) ∧
    (Function.Injective orbitMap ↔ PhonologicalCalculus.HasInverseOnRange orbitMap) ∧
      (PhonologicalCalculus.HasInverseOnRange orbitMap ↔ PhonologicalCalculus.HasTwoSidedInverseOnRange orbitMap)
PhonologicalCalculus.finite_query_factorization_on_reachable_image.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {X : Type u_1} {Strong : Type u_2}
  {Weak : Type u_3} [Fintype X] (strong : X → Strong) (weak : X → Weak) :
  (∃ g, ∀ (x : X), g ⟨strong x, ⋯⟩ = weak x) ↔ PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines strong weak
PhonologicalCalculus.firstLossAt_supplies_irreversible_witness.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4, u_5} {Index : Type u_1}
  {X : Type u_2} {State : Type u_3} {Answer : Type u_4} [LT Index] (stages : Index → X → State) (query : X → Answer)
  (index : Index) (hloss : PhonologicalCalculus.FirstLossAt stages query index) :
  ∃ x y,
    stages index x = stages index y ∧
      query x ≠ query y ∧
        ∀ (Output : Type u_5) (suffix : State → Output), suffix (stages index x) = suffix (stages index y)
PhonologicalCalculus.firstLossIndex_specification.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {Index : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {State : Type u_3} {Answer : Type u_4} [Fintype Index] [LinearOrder Index] (stages : Index → X → State)
  (query : X → Answer) (hloss : ∃ index, ¬PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines (stages index) query) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.FirstLossAt stages query (PhonologicalCalculus.firstLossIndex stages query hloss)
PhonologicalCalculus.inducedOrbitMap_mk.{u_1, u_2} {X : Type u_1} {Y : Type u_2} (source : Setoid X) (target : Setoid Y)
  (weakening : X → Y) (respects : PhonologicalCalculus.OrbitMapRespects source target weakening) (x : X) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.inducedOrbitMap source target weakening respects ⟦x⟧ = ⟦weakening x⟧
PhonologicalCalculus.injective_iff_all_consumers_preserved.{u, v} {X : Type u} {S : Type v} (reduce : X → S) :
  Function.Injective reduce ↔ ∀ (O : Type u) (consumer : X → O) ⦃x y : X⦄, reduce x = reduce y → consumer x = consumer y
PhonologicalCalculus.inverseOnRange_unique.{u_1, u_2} {X : Type u_1} {Y : Type u_2} (f : X → Y)
  (first second : ↑(Set.range f) → X) (hFirst : ∀ (x : X), first ⟨f x, ⋯⟩ = x)
  (hSecond : ∀ (x : X), second ⟨f x, ⋯⟩ = x) : first = second
PhonologicalCalculus.irreversible_deterministic_loss.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {X : Type u_1} {S : Type u_2} {Q : Type u_3}
  {O : Type u_4} (reduction : X → S) (query : X → Q) (suffix : S → O) {x y : X} (hcollision : reduction x = reduction y)
  (hdistinction : query x ≠ query y) :
  suffix (reduction x) = suffix (reduction y) ∧ ¬∃ reader, query = reader ∘ reduction
PhonologicalCalculus.irreversible_markov_loss.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {X : Type u_1} {S : Type u_2} {Q : Type u_3}
  {O : Type u_4} (reduction : X → S) (query : X → Q) (kernel : S → O → ℚ) {x y : X}
  (hcollision : reduction x = reduction y) (hdistinction : query x ≠ query y) :
  kernel (reduction x) = kernel (reduction y) ∧ ¬∃ reader, query = reader ∘ reduction
PhonologicalCalculus.kernelRefines_productQuery_iff.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {I : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {Reduction : Type u_3} {Answer : I → Type u_4} (reduction : X → Reduction)
  (queries : (index : I) → X → Answer index) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines reduction (PhonologicalCalculus.ProductQuery queries) ↔
    ∀ (index : I), PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines reduction (queries index)
PhonologicalCalculus.missing_core_field_refuses_carrier (checklist : PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseFormationChecklist)
  (hMissing :
    checklist.codeCarrier = false ∨
      checklist.interpretation = false ∨
        checklist.semanticEquality = false ∨
          checklist.reachableImage = false ∨
            checklist.reader = false ∨ checklist.descent = false ∨ checklist.commutingSquares = false) :
  checklist.carrierReady = false
PhonologicalCalculus.missing_section_preserves_carrier_decision
  (checklist : PhonologicalCalculus.ResponseFormationChecklist) (hCore : checklist.carrierReady = true)
  (hSection : checklist.sourceSection = false) : checklist.carrierReady = true ∧ checklist.sourceWitnessReady = false
PhonologicalCalculus.prefixStoppedProjection_refused
  (castRegistry : PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag → PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag → Bool)
  (hMissing :
    castRegistry PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.allPrefixes PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.stoppedSet = false) :
  ¬PhonologicalCalculus.SerialComparisonAdmitted castRegistry PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.allPrefixes
      PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.stoppedSet
PhonologicalCalculus.productQuery_kernel_exact.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {I : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {Answer : I → Type u_3}
  (queries : (index : I) → X → Answer index) (x y : X) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.ProductQuery queries x = PhonologicalCalculus.ProductQuery queries y ↔
    ∀ (index : I), queries index x = queries index y
PhonologicalCalculus.productQuery_minimum_carrier_cardinality.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {I : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {Reduction : Type u_3} {Answer : I → Type u_4} [Fintype X] (reduction : X → Reduction)
  (queries : (index : I) → X → Answer index)
  (hpreserves : ∀ (index : I), PhonologicalCalculus.KernelRefines reduction (queries index)) :
  Nat.card ↑(Set.range (PhonologicalCalculus.ProductQuery queries)) ≤ Nat.card ↑(Set.range reduction)
PhonologicalCalculus.product_query_kernel_exact.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {X : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2} {B : Type u_3}
  (first : X → A) (second : X → B) (x y : X) :
  (first x, second x) = (first y, second y) ↔ first x = first y ∧ second x = second y
PhonologicalCalculus.range_empty_of_isEmpty.{u_1, u_2} {X : Type u_1} {Y : Type u_2} [IsEmpty X] (f : X → Y) :
  Set.range f = ∅
PhonologicalCalculus.redundancyClosure_extensive.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Consumer : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {Answer : Consumer → Type u_3} (consumerUniverse : Finset Consumer)
  (answers : (consumer : Consumer) → X → Answer consumer) (battery : Finset Consumer)
  (hBattery : battery ⊆ consumerUniverse) :
  battery ⊆ PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers battery
PhonologicalCalculus.redundancyClosure_idempotent.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Consumer : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {Answer : Consumer → Type u_3} (consumerUniverse : Finset Consumer)
  (answers : (consumer : Consumer) → X → Answer consumer) (battery : Finset Consumer)
  (hBattery : battery ⊆ consumerUniverse) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers
      (PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers battery) =
    PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers battery
PhonologicalCalculus.redundancyClosure_monotone.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Consumer : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {Answer : Consumer → Type u_3} (consumerUniverse : Finset Consumer)
  (answers : (consumer : Consumer) → X → Answer consumer) {smaller larger : Finset Consumer}
  (hSubset : smaller ⊆ larger) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers smaller ⊆
    PhonologicalCalculus.RedundancyClosure consumerUniverse answers larger
PhonologicalCalculus.representativeRecovery_iff.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {A : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {Y : Type u_3}
  (weakening : X → Y) (sourceAction : A → X → X) (targetAction : A → Y → Y) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.RepresentativeRecoveryExists weakening sourceAction targetAction ↔
    Function.Injective weakening ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.TargetImageStable weakening targetAction ∧
        PhonologicalCalculus.ActionLiftCoherent weakening sourceAction targetAction
PhonologicalCalculus.sectionOnRange_unique_of_injective.{u_5, u_6} {Source : Type u_5} {Code : Type u_6}
  (encode : Source → Code) (hinjective : Function.Injective encode) (first second : Code → Source)
  (hFirst : PhonologicalCalculus.IsSectionOn encode (Set.range encode) first)
  (hSecond : PhonologicalCalculus.IsSectionOn encode (Set.range encode) second) (code : Code) :
  code ∈ Set.range encode → first code = second code
PhonologicalCalculus.section_lifts_every_code.{u_5, u_6} {Source : Type u_5} {Code : Type u_6} (encode : Source → Code)
  (domain : Set Code) (sectionMap : Code → Source)
  (hsection : PhonologicalCalculus.IsSectionOn encode domain sectionMap) (code : Code) :
  code ∈ domain → ∃ source, encode source = code
PhonologicalCalculus.serialAnswer_constructors_disjoint.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Word : Type u_1} {Coefficient : Type u_2}
  {Prefix : Type u_3} (stopped : Finset Word) (law : Word → ℚ) (infinityMass : ℚ) (series : ℕ → Coefficient)
  (prefixes : Finset Prefix) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.stoppedSet stopped ≠
      PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.stoppedProbability law infinityMass ∧
    PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.stoppedSet stopped ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.weightedSeries series ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.stoppedSet stopped ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.allPrefixes prefixes ∧
        PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.stoppedProbability law infinityMass ≠
            PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.weightedSeries series ∧
          PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.stoppedProbability law infinityMass ≠
              PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.allPrefixes prefixes ∧
            PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.weightedSeries series ≠
              PhonologicalCalculus.SerialAnswer.allPrefixes prefixes
PhonologicalCalculus.serialTags_pairwise_distinct :
  PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.stoppedSet ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.stoppedProbability ∧
    PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.stoppedSet ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.weightedSeries ∧
      PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.stoppedSet ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.allPrefixes ∧
        PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.stoppedProbability ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.weightedSeries ∧
          PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.stoppedProbability ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.allPrefixes ∧
            PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.weightedSeries ≠ PhonologicalCalculus.SerialTag.allPrefixes
PhonologicalCalculus.stoppedProbability_retains_nontermination {first second : PhonologicalCalculus.UnaryStoppedLaw}
  (hInfinity : first.infinityMass ≠ second.infinityMass) : first ≠ second
PhonologicalCalculus.strongPartialCongruence_intersection.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {Operation : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {Index : Type u_3} [Nonempty Index] (operations : Operation → X → Option X) (relations : Index → X → X → Prop)
  (hStrong : ∀ (index : Index), PhonologicalCalculus.StrongPartialCongruence operations (relations index)) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.StrongPartialCongruence operations (PhonologicalCalculus.RelationIntersection relations)
PhonologicalCalculus.stronger_query_preservation_implies_weaker.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {X : Type u_1} {Strong : Type u_2}
  {Weak : Type u_3} (strong : X → Strong) (weak : X → Weak) (g : Strong → Weak) (hfactor : weak = g ∘ strong) {x y : X}
  (hstrong : strong x = strong y) : weak x = weak y
PhonologicalCalculus.weak_preservation_does_not_imply_strong :
  PhonologicalCalculus.constantQuery PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.left =
      PhonologicalCalculus.constantQuery PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.right ∧
    PhonologicalCalculus.identityQuery PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.left ≠
      PhonologicalCalculus.identityQuery PhonologicalCalculus.ConverseWitness.right
PhonologicalCalculus.weightedSeries_admitsProbability_iff_normalized (coefficients : List ℚ) :
  PhonologicalCalculus.AdmitsProbabilitySemantics coefficients ↔
    PhonologicalCalculus.WeightedSeriesNormalized coefficients
